Result: 没有单一的搜索结构在所有任务上始终最高效；搜索效率与最终结果质量是独立的性能维度——最终最优的方法可能收敛缓慢且消耗更多预算；流体搜索在多个任务上实现了最高的整体搜索效率，接近每个任务预先知晓最优搜索结构的oracle性能。

Conclusion: 搜索效率应作为AI自主研究系统的重要评估维度；流体搜索作为一种自适应策略，能在未知最优搜索结构的场景下有效提升效率。

Abstract: AI-driven autonomous research (AR) systems are becoming increasingly effective across a broad range of tasks. Their performance, however, is still evaluated primarily by the quality of the final outcome. In this paper, we argue that the efficiency of the solution-search process is an equally important but often overlooked dimension of performance. A strong AR system should not only produce high-quality results, but also reach them with as small a budget as possible. Search efficiency will become increasingly important as AR expands from domains with inexpensive verification, such as mathematics and coding, to real-world scientific settings in which solution evaluation may require costly physical experiments. To capture this dimension, we propose evaluating AR systems using the area under the curve (AUC) of the Pareto frontier, alongside final outcome quality. We compare several families of search algorithms, including hill climbing, beam search, tree search, and evolutionary search, across twelve systems-optimization tasks. We find that no single search structure is consistently the most efficient. We also show that search efficiency and final outcome quality are distinct performance dimensions: a method that eventually achieves the best result may nevertheless improve slowly and consume substantially more evaluation budget before reaching that result. Because the most effective search policy is generally unknown in advance, we introduce an adaptive procedure called fluid search, which uses a portfolio bandit to dynamically allocate a fixed evaluation budget across a forest of search processes. Across the evaluated tasks, fluid search achieves the highest overall search efficiency, closely matching the performance of a per-task oracle that is given the best search structure for each task in advance.

</details>


### [263] [Reason-Mediated Behavioral Models for Auditing LLM Social Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24649)
*Atharva Pandey,Gautam Jajoo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过"推理状态（reason states）"对大型语言模型作为社会模拟器（如合成调查受访者）进行审计，发现LLM虽然能匹配人类最终答案，但其推理过程往往不可靠：人类推理有助于预测行为，而LLM模拟的推理常照搬产品描述而非真实的接受/拒绝路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注LLM模拟结果是否匹配人类行为，但这过于宽松——模拟器可能答对最终答案却使用错误的推理过程。需要更严格的审计框架来检验模拟器内部推理是否与人类一致。

Method: 开展94人防晒霜概念测试，每位受访者评估3个产品概念并撰写开放式推理。将推理映射为有符号的推理状态Z（正号支持采纳，负号阻止采纳）。固定受访者描述D、类别上下文K、概念处理X不变，比较人类推理和LLM模拟推理对行为Y的预测能力。

Result: 人类推理显著提升对购买意向的保留预测；LLM模拟的推理更脆弱，虽听起来合理但常照搬概念板而非恢复受访者的真实接受/拒绝路径。

Conclusion: 推理状态本身不能识别自然因果效应，但提供了可解释的测试来检验模拟器的推理是否与人类证据一致。本文为社会模拟器贡献了一个评估框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as social simulators, including as synthetic survey respondents. Most evaluations ask whether simulated outcomes resemble human outcomes. We argue that this is necessary but too weak: a simulator can match the final answer while using the wrong rationale-derived reason pattern. We study this problem through a 94-person sunscreen concept test in which each respondent evaluated three product concepts and wrote open-ended rationales. We map those rationales into signed reason states $Z$, where positive signs support adoption and negative signs block it. This gives a practical audit: holding respondent descriptors $D$, category context $K$, and concept treatment $X$ fixed, do human rationale-derived reasons help predict behavior $Y$, and can an LLM simulate the same reason state without seeing the human rationale or outcome? Human rationale-derived reasons substantially improve held-out prediction of purchase intent. LLM-simulated reasons are more brittle: they often sound plausible, but frequently echo the concept board rather than recover the respondent's acceptance or rejection path. The paper contributes an evaluation framework for social simulators. Reason states do not identify natural causal effects by themselves, but they provide an interpretable test of whether a simulator's stated reasons align with human evidence.

</details>


### [264] [Eviction as Estimation: A Fixed-Lag Smoothing View of Test-Time Memory, and When Measuring Beats Accumulating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24667)
*Maruthi Vemula,Neeraj Praneeth Gajula*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言模型的键值缓存驱逐问题重新定义为固定滞后的平滑估计问题，通过"demonstrated utility"（即观察模型近未来是否实际关注某token）作为信号来决策。实例化方法RMM在可控设置（重用内生且时间分离）中表现优越，但在独立第三方基准测试中与H2O持平或落后，原因在于自然文本中模型对大多数token的预测正确，使得"demonstrated utility"与累计注意力权重趋于一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存驱逐方法（如StreamingLLM、H2O、SnapKV）均在token到达时立即决策（在线过滤）或基于对未来猜测决策，但缺乏一个中等延迟的估计方案。作者希望将驱逐决策重新建模为信号估计问题，填补在线过滤（H=0）与Belady离线最优（全知未来）之间的空白，即固定滞后平滑（fixed-lag smoothing）——等待有限步数后利用模型自身的注意力信息再做决策。

Method: 提出RMM（Recency & Measurement Merge）策略：等待一个有限滞后窗口H，观测模型在后续步骤中实际关注了哪些token（demonstrated utility），然后基于此信息决定是否保留。这是对H2O的严格泛化——当demonstrated utility退化为均匀分布时即还原为H2O。该策略无需训练，仅依赖模型自身的前向计算。

Result: 在可控实验（重用内生于生成过程且时间上分离）中，demonstrated utility能比累积注意力权重更好地识别有用记忆，使小尺寸记忆表现近似大尺寸记忆。但在独立第三方基准（KVPress框架下的SnapKV、H2O、StreamingLLM实现）中，RMM在单轮问答上与H2O持平，在多轮流式场景中逊于H2O和SnapKV。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于提供了一个理论框架和对不同方法适用条件的诚实评估：当token重用的模式是内生的且与时间隔离时（如某些合成任务），基于近未来观测的驱逐策略显著优于基于历史累积信息的策略；但在标准自然语言基准中，模型对大多数token的预测置信度高，导致两种方法差异不大。本文并非追求SOTA，而是绘制了"何时测量优于累积"的边界地图。

Abstract: A language model with a bounded working memory must repeatedly decide which stored items to keep. Every deployed method decides the moment an item arrives, from the past (StreamingLLM, H2O) or from a guess about the future (SnapKV). We recast the choice as an estimation problem on a hidden signal, whether an item will be reused, placing existing methods on one axis, the commit lag $H$: online filters and learned predictors commit at $H=0$, while Belady's offline optimum sits where the whole future is known. The missing regime in between, fixed-lag smoothing, waits a bounded number of steps, observes which items a correct near-future prediction attended to, and only then commits. This measurement, demonstrated utility, turns Belady's unobservable future request into something we read off the model itself. We instantiate it as a training-free policy, RMM, a strict generalization of H2O that reduces to it exactly when the measurement is uniform. In controlled settings where reuse is endogenous and separated in time, demonstrated utility identifies used memory far better than accumulated attention, and a small bounded memory behaves like a much larger one. But on independent third-party benchmarks, run inside NVIDIA's KVPress harness against its own SnapKV, H2O, and StreamingLLM implementations, the advantage mostly disappears: RMM is on par with H2O for single-turn question answering and loses to both H2O and SnapKV in a streaming multi-turn setting. The cause is simple: on natural text the model is correct about most tokens, so weighting attention by correctness barely changes it, and demonstrated utility collapses onto accumulated attention unless reuse is sharp and endogenous, which standard benchmarks do not exercise. Our contribution is the framework and an honest map of when measuring beats accumulating, not a new state of the art.

</details>


### [265] [ERUnderstand: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Structured ER Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24707)
*Ali Ansari,Yasmin Mohammadi,Farnoush Nili,Parsa Esmaeilkhani,Longin Jan Latecki,Eduard Dragut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ERUnderstand——首个大规模ER图结构化理解基准，包含2,960张涵盖多种符号、复杂度和EER构造的图表，评估发现现有VLM在弱实体、多值属性和N元关系上表现较差，推理增强模型虽有提升但仍受语言先验和复杂度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ER图通常以渲染图像形式存在而非机器可读模式，限制了AI辅助数据库工程。现有缺乏标准化的ER图结构化理解基准。

Method: 构建包含2,960张ER图的基准数据集，来源包括教育资料、真实模式及合成样本，涵盖多种符号体系、复杂度及EER构造，每张图配有标准化机器可读表示。

Result: VLM对常见ERD元素恢复良好(F1>0.74)，但在弱实体(0.28 F1)、多值属性(0.14 F1)和N元关系(0.07 F1)上表现骤降；推理增强模型整体提升15-25%，但仍对语言先验和复杂度敏感。

Conclusion: ERUnderstand为多模态理解概念数据库模式提供了标准化基准，数据集、评估工具和生成代码已开源。

Abstract: Entity-Relationship Diagrams (ERDs) are central to conceptual database design, yet they are typically available only as rendered images rather than machine-readable schemas, limiting AI-assisted database engineering. We introduce ERUnderstand, the first large-scale benchmark for structured understanding of ER diagrams, comprising 2,960 diagrams collected from curated educational sources, real-world schemas, and synthetically generated examples spanning diverse domains, notations, complexity levels, and Extended Entity-Relationship (EER) constructs. Each diagram is paired with a standardized machine-readable representation for fine-grained evaluation of schema elements. Evaluating state-of-the-art Vision-Language Models (VLMs), we find that while common ERD elements are recovered reliably (F1 > 0.74), performance drops sharply on weak entities (as low as 0.28 F1), multivalued attributes (0.14 F1), and N-ary relationships (0.07 F1). Reasoning-augmented models improve overall performance by 15-25% but remain sensitive to linguistic priors and increasing diagram complexity. ERUnderstand provides a standardized benchmark for evaluating multimodal understanding of conceptual database schemas. The benchmark, dataset, evaluation toolkit, and generation code are publicly available at https://github.com/salinaria/ERUnderstand.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [266] [ReCon: A Resource-Constrained Benchmark for LLM-Based Cybersecurity Compliance Across Ingestion and Retrieval Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22885)
*Rohit Negi,Rishik Jain,Soumyo V Chakarborty,Amit Negi,Sandeep K Shukla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源受限环境下（无需GPU和高内存）使用轻量级LLM进行信息安全策略合规性检查的可行性，通过ISO 27002:2022标准对多个策略文档进行实验，证明低资源LLM在合规检查中能达到良好的一致性/准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全治理失败是导致安全态势薄弱的主要原因之一，而策略文档常存在与行业标准或法规之间的差距。人工识别这些差距需要大量精力，现有基于LLM的自动化方案依赖昂贵GPU和高内存服务器，中小组织难以负担，因此需要探索资源受限LLM的合规检查能力。

Method: 在不依赖GPU和高内存的资源受限环境下部署轻量级LLM，以ISO 27002:2022控制项为基准，对多个信息安全策略文档进行合规性检查实验，评估低资源LLM与标准/法规之间的一致性。

Result: 实验表明，低资源LLM在策略文档与标准的合规检查中能够提供良好的一致性/准确率。

Conclusion: 资源受限的LLM可以作为中小组织进行自动化合规检查的可行方案，降低了对昂贵计算资源的依赖。

Abstract: With the increasingly aggressive cyber threat landscape for governments, businesses, and institutions, as information and/or cybersecurity implementations are increasingly under scrutiny by regulators, it has been pointed out that governance failure is one of the major reasons for a weakened cybersecurity posture. A major component of Cyber/information security governance is the development, adoption, and implementation of a comprehensive information and/or cyber security policy document. The policy document must be in compliance with international or national standards and, if possible, with regulatory guidelines. However, it is often observed that policy documents are often incomplete with respect to industry standards or regulations and require revision when subjected to a thorough audit. Identifying the gaps between the controls and processes documented in the policy and those required in the regulations or standards necessitates extensive manual effort. The advent of Generative AI tools such as Large Language Models (LLMs) led to use of LLMs and Agentic AI tools to automate such compliance checks, as seen in a few research publications in recent times. However, such reported use of LLMs are experimented with high resource environments such as expensive GPUs and memory based servers. For smaller organizations such expensive compute platform may not be easily available. In this article, we benchmark the compliance checking tasks on LLMs that do not require GPU and high memory usage and the effectiveness of such resource constrained LLMs in compliance checking. Our experiments demonstrated that the low resource LLMs can provide good agreement/accuracy in compliance checking of policy documents against standards by experimenting with ISO 27002:2022 controls against multiple policy documents.

</details>


### [267] [A Modular Cyber Range Platform for Smart Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22943)
*Vasilis Ieropoulos,Theofanis Eleftheriadis,Kyriakos Christou,Philippos Isaia,Maria Michalopoulou,Angelos K. Marnerides,Christos Laoudias,Maria K. Michael*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级模块化网络靶场（CR），利用FastAPI后端和QEMU/KVM虚拟化技术，通过YAML引擎自动部署IT/OT融合网络。以光伏电站遭受多阶段网络攻击（从物联网设备入侵到军事基地断电）为例，验证其对Modbus/TCP、MQTT等工业协议的支持，并包含评估人员应急表现的"绿队"框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施需要弥合数字漏洞与物理后果之间差距的实用培训工具，现有方案缺乏轻量级、高保真且支持IT/OT融合仿真的解决方案。

Method: 基于FastAPI框架构建后端，采用QEMU/KVM虚拟化技术实现模块化架构；设计YAML引擎实现复杂网络的自动部署；集成Modbus/TCP和MQTT等工业协议仿真；构建"绿队"框架用于评估危机中的人员表现。

Result: 在标准硬件上以最小开销成功实现高保真IT/OT环境仿真，并通过光伏电站多阶段攻击场景（从家庭物联网设备失陷到军事基地断电）验证了平台能力。

Conclusion: 该网络靶场为关键基础设施培训提供了轻量化、可扩展的解决方案，支持工业协议原生仿真和人员绩效评估，有效连接了数字攻击与物理后果的培训需求。

Abstract: Modern critical infrastructure requires realistic training tools that bridge the gap between digital vulnerabilities and physical consequences. This paper introduces a lightweight, modular Cyber Range (CR) designed to simulate Information Technology (IT) and Operational Technology (OT) environments with high fidelity. Using the FastAPI framework as backend and Quick Emulator (QEMU)/Kernel-based Virtual Machine (KVM) virtualisation, the platform employs a YAML-based engine to automatically deploy complex networks on standard hardware with minimal overhead. To demonstrate its capabilities, we present a Photovoltaic (PV) Plant scenario set in a fictional smart city. The scenario follows a multi-stage attack that begins with the compromise of a home Internet of Things (IoT) device and escalates into a major power outage at a military base. The proposed CR, natively supports industrial protocols such as Modbus/TCP and Message Queuing Telemetry Transport (MQTT) and includes a "Green Team" framework for evaluating human performance during crisis situations. This paper describes the system architecture, automated setup, protocol-simulation capabilities, and scenario design.

</details>


### [268] [Practical Post-Quantum Cryptography for Bandwidth Constrained or Non-Terrestrial Networks, and Power Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23007)
*Elliot Eichen,Sylvia Llosa,Yueqi Chen,Sangtae Ha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用共享密钥（SSK）替代PQ数字证书的认证框架，结合KDC与预共享密钥PQ握手（如DTLS-PSK）及临时PQ密钥建立（如ML-KEM），显著降低带宽、内存、计算和能耗开销，适用于NTN、UAV、嵌入式医疗传感器等资源受限场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PQ认证算法（基于证书）在NTN及资源受限网络中开销过大（带宽、内存、计算、能耗），导致握手可靠性低、仅支持低安全等级，甚至无法支持PQ认证。

Method: 提出基于共享密钥（SSK）的认证框架，利用共享密钥生态（如5G/6G），结合KDC（如Kerberos）、预共享密钥PQ握手（DTLS-PSK）和临时PQ密钥建立（ML-KEM），替代传统基于证书的PQ数字证书认证。

Result: 相比基于证书的PQ认证（如ML-DSA），所提方法降低了握手带宽、端点RAM、计算时间和能耗，同时保持PQ安全的AEAD、前向安全性和重放抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 基于共享密钥的PQ认证框架可有效解决NTN及资源受限网络中的PQ认证开销问题，应用场景包括NTN密钥轮换管理、UAV控制、嵌入式医疗传感器及供应链监控等。

Abstract: Post-quantum (PQ) cryptographic algorithms, particularly for authentication, are more complex than classical algorithms and require larger certificates, signatures, and keys. Establishing a PQ-secure network connection increases bandwidth, memory, computation time, and energy consumption. These costs are especially severe in Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTNs), where long propagation delays, intermittent connectivity, constrained link budgets, satellite handovers, limited terminal resources, and bandwidth-constrained satellite-to-ground links amplify the overhead of certificate-based PQ authentication. Consequently, applications such as key rotation and key management may be unable to achieve acceptable handshake reliability or support NIST PQ Security Categories above Category 1. Similar limitations affect low-power IoT devices and bandwidth- or energy-constrained terrestrial networks, where PQ authentication may restrict devices to Category 1 security or prevent ambient-powered endpoints from supporting PQ authentication altogether.
  This paper investigates an alternative cryptographic framework that replaces PQ digital certificates with shared secret keys (SSKs). The framework leverages shared-secret ecosystems that do not rely on asymmetric key distribution, such as 5G/6G, and combines a Key Distribution Center (KDC) (e.g., Kerberos) with a preshared-key PQ handshake (e.g., DTLS-PSK) and ephemeral PQ key establishment (e.g., ML-KEM). Compared with certificate-based PQ authentication (e.g., ML-DSA), the proposed approach reduces handshake bandwidth, endpoint RAM, computation time, and energy use while preserving PQ-secure AEAD, including forward secrecy and replay resistance. Applications include NTN-based key rotation and management, uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) command-and-control systems, embedded medical sensors, and supply-chain monitoring and asset-tracking platforms.

</details>


### [269] [Mask2Shield: Strengthening LLM Safety against Neuron-Pruning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23015)
*Ying JinCheng,Minghui Xu,Yinhao Xiao,Xiuzhen Cheng,Wencheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Mask2Shield (M2S)方法，通过掩码前向对齐训练使模型安全行为分散到更多神经元中，从而降低针对安全神经元的小规模剪枝攻击成功率，在10个模型配置上将成功攻击数从80-279降至1-44。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在部署前会进行安全对齐以减少有害内容生成，但神经元级剪枝攻击表明，拒绝安全行为可能依赖于一小部分可移除的单元：禁用这些单元会消除安全行为，同时保留模型大部分可用功能。这暴露了安全对齐的脆弱性，需要增强模型对剪枝攻击的鲁棒性。

Method: Mask2Shield (M2S)是一种掩码前向对齐方法。该方法在功能剪枝条件下训练模型：掩码后的学生模型必须通过剩余的计算恢复安全拒绝行为，同时冻结的、未掩码的教师模型提供完整的良性回答以限制能力漂移。

Result: 在10个模型配置上，M2S将成功的重计算剪枝攻击从80-279降低至1-44（共313个提示）。同时，M2S在四个能力基准测试上基本保持了模型性能。此外，使用不同的神经元选择规则和迭代剪枝过程TwinBreak进行验证，结果一致。

Conclusion: M2S通过减少对一小部分可移除安全神经元集的依赖，使得针对性的剪枝攻击效果降低，从而有效增强了模型对安全神经元剪枝攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are safety-aligned before deployment to reduce harmful content generation. Yet neuron-level pruning attacks show that refusal can depend on a small set of removable units: disabling them can remove safety behavior while leaving much of the model usable. To address this problem, we introduce Mask2Shield (M2S), a masked-forward alignment method that trains a model under this functional pruning. The masked student must recover a safe refusal through the remaining computation, while a frozen, unmasked teacher supplies complete benign answers to limit capability drift. Across ten model configurations, M2S reduces successful recomputed pruning attacks from 80--279 to 1--44 out of 313 prompts while generally preserving four capability benchmarks. We also evaluate M2S with TwinBreak, which uses a different neuron-selection rule and iterative pruning procedure. Together, these results show that M2S makes targeted pruning less effective by reducing reliance on a small, removable safety-neuron set.

</details>


### [270] [GLST: Defending Confidence-Driven V2X Collaborative Perception Against Stealthy Multi-Attacker Feature Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23059)
*Ji He,Ying Wang,Lijie Zheng,Xinghui Zhu,Yulong Shen,Xiaohong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对基于置信度驱动的稀疏协作感知系统（以Where2comm为例），揭示了“伪装良善”攻击的安全隐患——攻击者通过在高置信度或自我不确定区域注入隐蔽扰动，绕过现有信任防御。进一步指出单一一致性信号的防御脆弱性，并提出多层级结构信任机制（GLST），从全局特征一致性、多尺度局部残差一致性及语义拓扑结构一致性三个互补维度评估协作方可靠性。实验表明GLST在单攻击者和多攻击者场景下均显著优于现有单一信号防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度驱动的稀疏协作感知系统在提升通信效率的同时，引入了安全隐患：攻击者可能利用系统的空间置信度机制，在被优先选择和融合的高置信度或自我不确定区域注入恶意特征。同时，现有基于信任的防御主要依赖单一一致性信号，在多攻击者场景下易被伪共识欺骗。因此，需要设计更鲁棒的多层级信任评估机制。

Method: 提出Global-Local Structural Trust (GLST)防御方法，从三个互补视角评估协作方可靠性：(1) 全局特征一致性；(2) 多尺度局部残差一致性；(3) 与自我侧语义拓扑结构的一致性。综合三个视角得到信任分数，引导特征融合以抑制不可靠协作方的干扰。

Result: 在OPV2V数据集上，面对四攻击者的伪装良善攻击，GLST仍能保持0.69 AP@0.3的检测性能，而现有单一信号防御方法严重退化。GLST还对梯度攻击（如PGD）保持有效。

Conclusion: 多层级信任建模对于保障置信度驱动的协作感知安全至关重要；仅依赖单一一致性信号的防御存在根本性弱点。

Abstract: Collaborative perception (CP) improves autonomous-driving perception by enabling connected vehicles to exchange intermediate features via V2X. Confidence-driven sparse communication reduces bandwidth by transmitting only perception-critical spatial regions, but creates a security risk: once a collaborator is compromised, malicious features in high-confidence or ego-uncertain regions may be preferentially selected and amplified during fusion. Using Where2comm as a representative framework, we show that the proposed Pretend Benign attack exploits its spatial-confidence mechanism by injecting stealthy perturbations into uncertain yet perception-critical regions, substantially degrading 3D object detection while preserving benign-like feature characteristics. Beyond this attack-framework pair, we identify a broader weakness of existing trust-based defenses: their reliance primarily on a single consistency signal leaves them vulnerable when multiple attackers form a pseudo-consensus that biases trust estimation. We therefore propose Global-Local Structural Trust (GLST), a lightweight defense that assesses collaborator reliability through three complementary perspectives: global feature consistency, multi-scale local residual consistency, and structural consistency with ego-side semantic topology. The resulting trust scores guide feature fusion to suppress unreliable collaborators. Experiments on OPV2V show that GLST achieves competitive performance against single-attacker Pretend Benign attacks and substantially stronger robustness in multi-attacker settings. Under a four-attacker Pretend Benign attack, GLST maintains 0.69 AP@0.3, whereas existing single-signal defenses degrade severely. GLST also remains effective against gradient-based attacks such as PGD, indicating that multi-level trust modeling is essential for securing confidence-driven CP.

</details>


### [271] [Traceable LLM Reasoning for Fake-Order Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23075)
*Siqi You,Bingsong Xu,Zhixian Zheng,Xinjian Peng,Yang Xie,Ying Wang,Jiarong Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DeepScrub是一个基于大语言模型和强化学习的假订单欺诈检测框架，通过语义统一模块、领域知识注入和SURE迭代推理机制，在真实数据集上达到85.3%宏F1，并在实际部署中实现91.8%精度和88.5%召回率，减少94%的一审人工审核工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有假订单欺诈检测方法依赖专家设计的特征、产生黑盒决策且可解释性有限，无法满足大规模O2O服务平台的需求。

Method: 提出DeepScrub框架，包含三个创新：(1) 语义统一模块将异质风险信号转化为LLM可理解的文本描述；(2) 在风控语料上进行持续预训练注入领域知识，并用任务奖励联合评估预测正确性和推理质量；(3) SURE机制整合专家反馈和模型自检以迭代优化推理路径。

Result: 真实数据集上宏F1达85.3%，超越最佳基线2.7个百分点；任务优化的8B模型超越32B模型；四周期线上实验精度91.8%、召回88.5%，比一审人工审核分别提升16.6和38.8个百分点，减少94%一审工作量，每年节省约100万元。

Conclusion: DeepScrub在提升欺诈审核准确率的同时降低了一审审核工作量，并为生产环境的风险审核流程提供了可追溯的证据。

Abstract: Detecting fake-order fraud at scale remains a critical challenge for large online-to-offline (O2O) service platforms, as existing approaches often rely on expert-designed features, produce black-box decisions, and provide limited interpretability. To address these limitations, we propose DeepScrub, a reinforcement learning framework built upon large language models (LLMs) for fake-order fraud detection with traceable reasoning. DeepScrub introduces three innovations. First, a semantic unification module converts heterogeneous risk signals into textual descriptions that LLMs can understand. Second, continued pre-training on risk-control corpora injects domain knowledge, and task rewards jointly evaluate prediction correctness and reasoning quality. Third, the SUggest-REflect (SURE) mechanism incorporates expert feedback and model self-checking to iteratively refine reasoning paths. On a real-world fake-order fraud detection dataset, DeepScrub achieves a macro-F1 score of 85.3%, outperforming the best baseline by 2.7 percentage points. Our task-optimized 8B model further surpasses a 32B model, showing that domain adaptation can matter more than model scale in this setting. In a four-week live pilot, DeepScrub achieved 91.8% precision and 88.5% recall, improving over first-stage human reviewers by 16.6 and 38.8 percentage points. It reduced first-stage manual review workload by 94% and saved nearly one million RMB annually. These results show that DeepScrub improves fraud review accuracy, reduces first-stage review workload, and provides traceable evidence for production risk-review workflows.

</details>


### [272] [Poster: Rethinking Security in LLM Code Generation through Real-World Risk Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23088)
*Lixun Ma,Ruolong Ma,Bei Wang,Feng Wei,Zhenguang Liu,Lorenzo Cavallaro,Wentao Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文从开发者视角识别了三种导致LLM生成代码安全漏洞的风险场景（模糊需求、未明确的操作上下文、安全与功能冲突），构建了包含2700个测试用例的大规模基准，评估了8个最先进LLM，发现平均漏洞率超过56%，并证明安全感知提示可将风险降低最多45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖明确指定的安全需求，无法反映现实开发中提示词常模糊或不完整的场景，亟需从开发者视角系统研究LLM代码生成的安全行为。

Method: 识别三类代表性风险场景（模糊需求、未明确操作上下文、安全-功能冲突），构建包含2700个测试用例的大规模基准，对8个SOTA LLM进行细粒度安全评估。

Result: 所有评估模型在各种风险场景下的平均漏洞率超过56%；安全感知提示可将漏洞率降低最多45%。

Conclusion: LLM在真实开发场景下生成的代码存在显著安全漏洞，安全感知提示是一种有效的缓解手段。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used for code generation, yet their security behavior in realistic development workflows remains underexplored. Existing benchmarks often rely on explicitly specified security requirements, failing to capture real-world scenarios where prompts are frequently ambiguous or incomplete. In this paper, we adopt a developer-centric perspective and identify three representative risk scenarios that commonly lead to security vulnerabilities in LLM-generated code: Ambiguous Requirements, Under-Specified Operational Context, and Security--Functionality Conflict. Based on these scenarios, we construct a large-scale benchmark comprising 2,700 test cases, enabling fine-grained evaluation of LLM security under realistic conditions. Extensive evaluation of eight state-of-the-art LLMs reveals that all models exhibit average vulnerability rates exceeding 56\% across risk scenarios. We further demonstrate that security-aware prompting can substantially mitigate these risks, achieving up to 45\% improvement.

</details>


### [273] [False Prophets: On the Security of World Models in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23147)
*Erik Imgrund,Anna Wimbauer,Klim Kireev,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在智能体系统中使用世界模型（world models）的安全风险，发现了一系列世界模型特有的漏洞，可被利用在终端型智能体中执行恶意代码或提取敏感数据。攻击者能以高达95%的成功率诱导智能体管道产生错误预测，导致意外命令执行、拒绝服务、钱包被盗和隐私信息泄露。文章还构建了专门的安全基准数据集，并提供了缓解建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的自主智能体在执行复杂多步任务时需要预测行动结果，世界模型虽能提升预测能力，但也可能误导智能体执行有害行为，造成严重的安全和隐私风险。现有研究对此关注不足，本文旨在系统性地揭示这些风险并提出应对方案。

Method: 作者首先识别并分类了一系列世界模型特有的安全漏洞；然后构建了面向文本型世界模型的安全基准数据集；最后通过实验量化攻击成功率（最高达95%），并给出实用缓解建议。

Result: 攻击者可以诱导智能体管道产生错误预测，成功率达到95%，导致意外命令执行、拒绝服务、钱包资产流失和私人信息泄露等多种危害。

Conclusion: 近似世界建模本身具有内在风险，智能体系统在使用世界模型时需要格外关注安全防护。作者为从业者提供了缓解已发现危害和加固智能体系统的实用建议。

Abstract: Large language models now power autonomous agents capable of complex, multi-step tasks in different environments. Accurate and reliable execution of these tasks requires the agent to predict the results of its actions. Recent research proposes to enhance predictive capabilities via specially trained environment simulators-world models. While world models can improve performance, they can also mislead agents into executing harmful actions, creating significant security and privacy risks. In this paper, we raise security concerns regarding the usage of world models in agentic systems. We discover a range of world model specific vulnerabilities, which can be exploited in terminal-based agents to execute malicious code or extract sensitive data. To facilitate future development, we introduce a security benchmark dataset designed for text-based world models. We argue that some risks are intrinsic to approximate world modeling, and show that attackers can induce mispredictions in agentic pipelines with up to 95% success rate, possibly resulting in unintended command execution, denial of service, drainage of wallet and private information extraction. Finally, we provide practical recommendations for practitioners to mitigate the discovered harms and harden agentic systems.

</details>


### [274] [SPARC: Automated Root-Cause Analysis of Pre-Silicon Power Side-Channel Leakage in the Processor Design Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23218)
*Andrija Nešković,Christian Ewert,Mladen Berekovic,Saleh Mulhem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SPARC是一个基于宏单元级信息流跟踪（IFT）的自动化框架，用于前硅片阶段处理器功耗侧信道泄漏（PSCL）评估与根因分析，支持将泄漏归因到具体硬件信号与软件指令，并在多种RISC-V CPU和密码学负载上实现8倍仿真加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前硅片PSCL评估框架要么仿真扩展性差，要么无法将泄漏归因到正确的硬件信号和软件指令，阻碍了全面的根因分析。

Method: SPARC利用宏单元级信息流跟踪（IFT）配合增强的影子逻辑，标记源自秘密数据的开关活动，通过隔离该活动并应用统计泄漏测试检测PSCL，同时将泄漏归因到特定硬件信号并映射到对应软件指令。

Result: 在32位/64位、顺序/乱序多款开源RISC-V CPU上，针对掩码/未掩码AES和ML-KEM等工作负载，SPARC恢复了已知泄漏源，识别了特定微架构泄漏源，实现了8倍每迹仿真加速。

Conclusion: SPARC通过在前硅片阶段实现精确、可扩展的根因分析，提供了一个实用框架，能够在设计流程早期缓解PSCL，从而增强未来处理器的安全性。

Abstract: Power-Side-Channel Leakage (PSCL) originates from architectural and micro-architectural artifacts in a processor and poses a severe threat to the confidentiality of cryptographic software. Consequently, pre-silicon PSCL evaluation is indispensable for secure hardware design. Existing frameworks are either limited by poor simulation scalability or fail to attribute leakage to the correct hardware signals and software instructions, thereby impeding a comprehensive root-cause analysis. This paper presents SPARC, an automated framework for pre-silicon PSCL evaluation and root-cause analysis. SPARC leverages macro-cell-level Information Flow Tracking (IFT) augmented with enhanced shadow logic that tags switching activity originating from secret-dependent data. By isolating this activity, SPARC applies statistical leakage tests to detect PSCL, while simultaneously attributing the leakage to specific hardware signals and mapping those signals to the corresponding software instructions. This approach thus delivers a full end-to-end leakage evaluation and root-cause analysis for both hardware and software. To demonstrate and validate SPARC, PSCL of multiple open-source RISC-V CPUs, encompassing 32-bit and 64-bit cores with both in-order and out-of-order pipelines, is evaluated across a range of cryptographic workloads, including masked and unmasked AES and ML-KEM (CRYSTALS-Kyber-512). SPARC recovers known leakage sources as a sanity check and identifies specific microarchitectural leakage sources, achieving an 8x per-trace simulation speedup over previously shown approaches on comparable designs. By enabling precise and scalable root-cause analysis at the pre-silicon stage, this work provides a practical framework to mitigate PSCL early in the design flow, thereby strengthening the security of future processors.

</details>


### [275] [From Signals to Behaviors: Evidence-Based Android Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23272)
*Shiwen Song,Yiheng Xiong,Sen Chen,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Praxis，一种基于行为的安卓恶意软件检测方法，通过“假设-确认-判断”流水线：从粗粒度静态信号假设候选行为，用程序分析验证代码证据，并在用户意识、应用功能上下文和攻击组合的语境中判断行为。在三个挑战性场景下，F1达87.4%，比七种基线高18.6-34.8个百分点；高权限良性应用误报率降至13.0%，比基线降低41.1-67.0个百分点；细粒度恶意行为恢复F1达87.3%，比先前方法高56.5-73.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安卓恶意软件检测方法基于表面信号或代理特征（如学习特征、局部代码片段）进行检测，将偏离这些代理的行为标记为恶意。但偏离不等于恶意：良性应用被过度标记，而变种恶意软件却可绕过检测。因此需要一种真正面向行为的检测方法。

Method: 提出Praxis系统，采用“假设-确认-判断”三阶段流水线：(1) 假设阶段，从粗粒度静态信号推断候选恶意行为；(2) 确认阶段，通过程序分析在代码中验证每个行为的证据；(3) 判断阶段，在用户意识、应用功能上下文及行为组合成攻击的语境中判断行为的恶意性。

Result: 在三个挑战性设置下，Praxis达到87.4% F1，优于七种基线18.6-34.8个百分点；高权限良性应用误报率仅13.0%，比基线降低41.1-67.0个百分点；细粒度恶意行为恢复F1达87.3%，优于先前行为级方法56.5-73.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 面向行为的检测方法能有效区分真正的恶意行为与仅仅是异常的行为，显著降低误报率并提升对变种恶意软件的检测能力。消融实验表明流水线各阶段均对最终性能有贡献。

Abstract: Android malware remains a persistent threat, and detecting it accurately is a long-standing open problem. Whether an app is malicious depends on what it actually does and the context in which it does it, not on the surface signals it happens to exhibit. Existing detectors instead reason about proxies for behavior, such as learned features or local code slices, and flag whatever deviates from these proxies as malicious. But deviation is not maliciousness: benign apps that merely look unusual are over-flagged, evolving malware that looks ordinary slips through. We argue that detection should be behavior-oriented: recover an app's potentially malicious behaviors and judge which are truly malicious. To realize this, we present Praxis, which structures detection as a hypothesize-confirm-judge pipeline: it hypothesizes candidate behaviors from coarse static signals, confirms each by grounding it in code evidence verified with program analysis, and judges the confirmed behaviors in context: the user's awareness, the app's functional context, and how they compose into an attack. For a malicious app, Praxis returns a verdict and the supported behaviors. We evaluate Praxis against seven baselines across three challenging settings. It achieves the best overall detection performance (87.4% F1), outperforming the baselines by 18.6-34.8 percentage points. On high-permission benign apps, it reduces the false-positive rate to 13.0%, a reduction of 41.1-67.0 percentage points compared with the baselines. Beyond binary detection, Praxis recovers fine-grained malicious behaviors at 87.3% F1, outperforming prior behavior-level approaches by 56.5-73.4 percentage points. Ablation studies show that each stage of the pipeline contributes to the final performance.

</details>


### [276] [Hiding in Plain Sight: An Effective Physical Adversarial Patch Attack against Visual-Infrared Fused Face Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23292)
*Qiucheng Yu,Tao Ni,Yihe Zhou,Jiayimei Wang,Qingchuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VIPatch，一种针对可见光-红外融合人脸检测模型的物理对抗补丁攻击方法，通过联合优化渐变颜色面具和创可贴贴纸，在数字和物理域均实现90%以上的攻击成功率，且补丁外观自然不易察觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击大多仅针对可见光或红外单一模态进行数字域攻击，对融合模型在物理世界中的攻击效果不佳，且生成的补丁图案与真实世界差异大，容易被察觉。

Method: 提出VIPatch方法，设计渐变颜色面具（gradient-color mask）和创可贴贴纸（band-aid sticker）两种元素，在可见光和红外图像上联合优化这两个元素，生成的数字补丁进一步指导物理补丁的制作。

Result: 在数字域和物理域均实现超过90%的攻击成功率，且补丁对人类观察者来说不显眼、自然逼真。

Conclusion: VIPatch能够有效攻击可见光-红外融合人脸检测模型，在保持高攻击成功率的同时，生成的补丁具有视觉隐蔽性和物理可实现性。

Abstract: Deep learning-based visual-infrared fused face detection models are increasingly deployed across a wide range of applications, yet they remain susceptible to adversarial patch attacks. Most prior attacks target either the visual or the infrared image alone in the digital domain, which renders them ineffective against fused models in the physical world. Moreover, many of these methods are readily noticeable, as their patch patterns deviate substantially from those seen in the real world. In this paper, we introduce VIPatch (Visual-Infrared Patch), a novel physical adversarial patch attack that produces inconspicuous, realistic, and natural-looking patches for facial images. Specifically, VIPatch crafts a gradient-color mask together with a band-aid sticker across both the visual and infrared images, and jointly optimizes these two elements; the resulting digital patches further guide the fabrication of their physical counterparts. Experimental results show that VIPatch achieves competitive attack success rates (over 90%) in both the digital and physical domains, while keeping the patches unobtrusive to human observers.

</details>


### [277] [A Structured Cyber Threat Intelligence Dataset Using STIX 2.1 Entities and MITRE ATT&CK Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23312)
*Dipshikha Das,Arnab Banik,Md. Shariful Islam,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含150份英文CTI报告、以STIX 2.1格式标注的图谱数据集（4,777个实体、5,817个关系、1,273个攻击模式映射到269个ATT&CK技术），并评估了四种本地部署的开源LLM作为自动化评判器的性能，其中Qwen3.6:27B表现最优（Kappa=0.803，F1>92%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTI研究提供了提取工具、知识图谱框架和ATT&CK映射数据集，但缺乏能够保留复杂实体关系并规范化对抗行为的、经过人工标注的报告级数据集。

Method: 1) 人工构建150份英文CTI报告为STIX 2.1图谱（4,777实体、5,817关系、1,273攻击模式映射269个ATT&CK技术）；2) 25份报告由两名研究员独立评估并裁决定标；3) 评估四种本地部署开源LLM（Qwen3.6:27B等）作为自动化评判器的性能。

Result: Qwen3.6:27B表现最佳：最大Kappa系数0.803，微F1>92%，假阳性率<5%。

Conclusion: 该数据集可作为CTI信息抽取、知识图谱构建、事件分析和威胁归因的基准；本地部署的LLM可辅助人工审校识别标注不一致，但专家验证仍不可或缺。

Abstract: Cyber threat intelligence (CTI) reports are typically written in unstructured formats, which complicates the extraction and analysis of important entities and adversarial behaviors. Although existing CTI research provides extraction tools, knowledge-graph frameworks, and MITRE ATT&CK mapped datasets, curated report-level datasets that preserve complex entity relationships and normalized adversarial behaviors remain limited. To address this limitation, this study presents a manually constructed dataset of 150 English-language CTI reports, each represented as STIX 2.1 based graphs, which includes 4,777 STIX entities, 5,817 STIX relationships in total, and 1,273 STIX attack-pattern entities (adversarial behaviors) mapped to 269 unique MITRE ATT&CK Enterprise techniques and sub-techniques. Twenty five randomly sampled reports were independently assessed by two cybersecurity researchers, which shows substantial inter-rater agreement. Disagreements were subsequently adjudicated to establish a gold-standard reference dataset. Four locally deployed open-source LLMs were evaluated as automated judges against this adjudicated reference sample. Qwen3.6:27B achieved the strongest overall performance, with a maximum kappa score of 0.803, micro-F1 scores exceeding 92%, and false-positive rates below 5%. The dataset provides a benchmark for CTI information extraction, knowledge-graph construction, incident analysis, and threat attribution. The findings further indicate that locally deployed LLMs can support human reviewers in identifying annotation inconsistencies, but expert validation remains essential.

</details>


### [278] [Exploring the OODA Loop as a Systematic Way of Thinking in Coping with Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23342)
*Ethan Anderson,Shouhuai Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了博伊德的OODA循环（观察-定向-决策-行动），探讨其作为动态、不确定、对抗环境下冲突推理的系统性思维方法，并通过网络冲突和认知冲突两个领域验证其有效性，强调冲突的本质是对观察、定向、决策能力的生成、保护、利用与对抗性削弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当冲突出现时，需要系统性的思维方式来应对。现有方法在处理动态、不确定、自适应且存在对抗干扰的环境中的冲突时仍显不足，因此需要探索更有效的系统性推理框架。

Method: 重新审视OODA循环理论，将其从空中作战的原始语境扩展到网络冲突和认知冲突两个应用领域进行分析，重点强调态势感知作为定向的关键机制，并探讨观察的有效性取决于感知、理解、投射到未来可能性以及与心智模型、目标、条令、信任和经验的整合。

Result: 冲突不仅是行动或效果的较量，更是关于生成、保护、利用自身优越的观察、定向和决策能力，同时削弱和利用对手的观察、定向和决策能力的斗争。

Conclusion: OODA循环作为系统性思维方式，在网络冲突和认知冲突两个领域均有效，揭示了冲突的本质是对认知能力的争夺，而不仅仅是物理层面的对抗。

Abstract: When conflicts emerge, we need systematic ways of thinking to deal with them. This paper revisits Boyd's Observe, Orient, Decide, Act (OODA) loop and explores its usefulness as a systematic way of thinking for reasoning about conflicts in dynamic environments characterized by uncertainty, adaptation, and adversarial interference. We explore the OODA loop beyond its origin in air warfare where it focuses attention on the relationship between information, understanding, choice, and action. Our exploration is conducted in two application domains: cyber conflicts and cognitive conflicts. Across both domains, we emphasize situational awareness as a critical mechanism of orientation, while noting that observations become useful only when they are perceived, comprehended, projected into possible futures, and integrated with mental models, objectives, doctrine, trust, and experience. Our exploration suggests that conflict is not merely a contest of actions or effects, but a contest over the ability to generate, protect, and leverage one's own superior observation, orientation, and decision, while degrading and exploiting adversary's observation, orientation, and decision.

</details>


### [279] [Rendering on Real Silicon: GPU Render-Timing as a Passive, AI-Resistant CAPTCHA Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23389)
*David Noever,Forrest McKee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用GPU渲染时序行为作为CAPTCHA替代方案，通过测量WebGL渲染时间而非像素输出来区分人类与自动化程序。在12小时被动部署中检测到86%自动化流量，软件渲染自动化与真实GPU的渲染时间相差约5倍，即使在相同GPU和浏览器引擎下，headless自动化与人类操作仍有75-106%的时序特征差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CAPTCHA正被AI系统攻克，行为验证和密码学证明存在隐私或注册成本问题，亟需一种不泄露持久标识符且难以被自动化模拟的防御信号。

Method: 测量客户端在受控WebGL渲染工作负载下的物理时序行为（渲染时序动态），而非像素输出的设备指纹。通过单端点收集真实浏览器和关键headless自动化的GPU时序样本，进行比较分析。

Result: 软件渲染自动化与真实GPU平均渲染时间相差约5倍；相同GPU和浏览器引擎下，headless自动化与人类样本在帧抖动、计时器量化比和变异系数上分离75-106%。

Conclusion: GPU渲染时序特征可作为区分人类与自动化的有效信号，但当前仅为单一GPU架构的初步发现，需跨架构收集以验证泛化性。

Abstract: Conventional CAPTCHAs pose puzzles that modern AI systems increasingly solve, while behavioral and cryptographic-attestation defenses carry privacy or enrollment costs. We investigate an orthogonal signal: the physical timing behavior of a client's GPU under a controlled WebGL rendering workload. Unlike WebGL fingerprinting, which hashes pixel output into a static device identifier, we measure render-timing dynamics to classify rather than identify, leaking no persistent identifier. We characterize the in-the-wild adversary with a 12-hour passive deployment (207 unsolicited requests; 86% automated; 85% of browser-claiming clients failed HTTP header-consistency checks). We then collect labeled GPU-timing samples through a single public endpoint exercised by real browsers (positive class, 13 distinct GPUs) and by keyed headless automation across a render-backend matrix (negative class). Software-rendered automation -- empirically the dominant real-world adversary -- separates from genuine GPUs by roughly 5x in mean render time. On a confound-controlled comparison (identical GPU family and browser engine, differing only in headless vs. interactive execution), headless automation on real hardware still exhibits a distinct timing signature, separating from human samples by 75-106% on frame jitter, timer-quantization ratio, and coefficient of variation. We report these as pilot-scale findings on a single GPU architecture and outline the cross-architecture collection required to establish generalization.

</details>


### [280] [Early Detection of Hardware Trojans Using Neural Controlled Differential Equations and Analysis of Power Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23417)
*Hasala Senevirathne,Rahul Vishwakarma,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经控制微分方程（NCDE）和功耗轨迹分析的硬件木马检测与预测方法，利用仅在无木马数据上训练的NCDE模型学习正常功耗行为，结合线性判别分析（LDA）分类器区分无木马、休眠木马和激活木马三种状态，通过滑动窗口处理侧信道测量值，实现对休眠木马的有效检测。实验表明该方法优于传统机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化型硬件木马通过隐蔽的自适应行为逃避传统检测方法，即便是最新的机器学习方法也只能在木马激活后检测，存在安全窗口期。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现对休眠状态木马的提前检测与预测。

Method: 采用仅在无木马数据上训练的NCDE模型学习正常功耗行为，结合LDA分类器对标记数据进行校准，区分无木马、休眠木马和激活木马三种状态；使用滑动窗口处理侧信道测量值，捕捉微小的功耗偏差。

Result: 相比传统机器学习方法，NCDE方法在检测精度上更优，且能够检测灵敏度阈值以上的休眠木马；在标准硬件木马基准测试上验证了鲁棒的检测与预测性能。

Conclusion: 基于NCDE和功耗轨迹分析的方法能有效检测休眠状态和激活状态的硬件木马，缩小了传统方法只能检测激活木马的安全窗口，为硬件安全提供了一种更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Evolving Hardware Trojans pose a serious threat to modern digital systems by evading traditional detection through stealthy, adaptive behavior. Even recent methods that leverage advances in machine learning can only detect them after activation, leaving a critical window for potential security breaches. To address this gap, we propose a novel approach for hardware Trojan detection and prediction using Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs) and analysis of power traces. Our method leverages an NCDE model trained exclusively on Trojan-free data to learn nominal power behavior, combined with a Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA) classifier calibrated on labeled data, to distinguish between three scenarios: no Trojan, dormant Trojan, and active Trojan. Our method uses a sliding window to process side-channel measurements, enabling detection of subtle power consumption deviations that indicate Trojan presence, even when dormant. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed NCDE-based method achieves superior accuracy compared to traditional machine learning approaches, with the additional advantage of handling dormant Trojans above a sensitivity threshold. We validate our approach on standard hardware Trojan benchmarks, showing robust detection and prediction performance.

</details>


### [281] [TroPUF: Evaluating Hardware Trojan Insertion in Delay-Based Physical Unclonable Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23418)
*Marissa Marcarelli,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在延迟型物理不可克隆函数（PUF）内部嵌入硬件木马的安全风险，提出了一个统一的仿真框架来评估多种PUF架构和木马类型。结果表明，休眠木马能保持PUF的正常行为、结构特征和抗建模能力，只有在激活后才出现可检测的退化，传统验证方法无法在负载执行前识别嵌入式木马，揭示当前PUF安全假设存在漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟型PUF依赖制造引起的延迟变化，但同一变化使PUF本质上是非确定性的，可能使恶意逻辑混入正常电路行为。在PUF原语内部直接嵌入硬件木马是一种尚未被充分理解的独特安全风险。

Method: 提出了一个统一的仿真框架，跨多种延迟型PUF架构和木马类型评估隐蔽式硬件木马插入。并行评估功能指标、硬件开销和抗机器学习建模能力。

Result: 休眠木马能保持预期的PUF行为、结构特征和抗建模能力；可检测的退化仅在木马激活后出现，传统验证技术在负载执行前无法识别嵌入式木马。

Conclusion: 当前PUF安全假设存在漏洞，需要将PUF和硬件木马作为耦合安全问题加以评估。

Abstract: Delay-based Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs) are commonly used for device authentication and key generation due to the fact that they rely on manufacturing induced delay variations. However, these same variations make PUFs inherently non-deterministic, which can allow malicious logic to blend in with normal circuit behavior. As a result, the act of embedding hardware Trojans directly inside the PUF primitive presents a unique security risk that is not yet well understood. This work presents a unified simulation framework for evaluating stealthy hardware Trojan insertion across multiple delay-based PUF architectures and Trojan types. Functional metrics, hardware overhead, and resistance to machine learning modeling are assessed in parallel. Results show that dormant Trojans preserve expected PUF behavior, structural characteristics, and modeling resistance. Detectable degradation appears only after activation, indicating that conventional validation techniques fail to identify embedded Trojans prior to payload execution. These findings expose a gap in current PUF security assumptions, and highlight the need to evaluate PUFs and hardware Trojans as a coupled security problem.

</details>


### [282] [PATCH-FFT: Unmasking Dormant Hardware Trojans with Patch-Based Frequency-Domain Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23421)
*Hasala Senevirathne,Amin Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于补丁的Transformer架构，通过频域分析(rFFT)检测功率侧信道中处于休眠状态的硬件木马，在休眠和激活场景下平均检测准确率达90.94%，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息泄露型硬件木马在触发前处于休眠状态，一旦被激活秘密数据已被窃取。现有方法主要关注激活态木马检测，难以检测休眠态木马。

Method: 采用补丁式Transformer架构，将功率迹的时域测量值通过实数快速傅里叶变换(rFFT)转换为频域表示，捕捉传统时域分析无法发现的频谱特征。

Result: 在休眠和激活木马场景下平均检测准确率达90.94%，在休眠木马检测任务上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效检测休眠态硬件木马，填补了现有方法在休眠检测方面的空白，且对激活态木马同样有效。

Abstract: Hardware Trojans embedded by malicious entities in integrated circuits can covertly leak sensitive information through power side channels, often remaining undetected in their dormant state until specific trigger conditions activate their malicious behavior. For information-leaking Trojans, detection in the dormant state is critical, as once triggered, the secret data is already exfiltrated. This paper introduces a patch-based Transformer architecture for detecting dormant hardware Trojans through frequency-domain analysis of power traces. Our approach converts time-domain power measurements into frequency-domain representations using real Fast Fourier Transform (rFFT), revealing spectral signatures hidden in conventional time-series analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves 90.94% average detection accuracy across dormant and active Trojan scenarios, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches particularly in detecting dormant Trojans that prior time-domain methods do not address.

</details>


### [283] [Renting the Cracking Machine with a Cost-and-Time Analysis of Exhaustive DES-56 Key Search in the Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23443)
*Gonzalo Sharif Curi Martínez,Rodrigo Ramele*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于AWS EC2云基础设施构建分布式暴力破解系统，使用37个c6i.2xlarge实例对DES的56位密钥空间进行穷举搜索，实测聚合吞吐量为1.08亿密钥/秒。小偏移量攻击（≤10^8）平均耗时116.8秒，成本0.41美元；完全穷举2^56密钥空间预计耗时约21年（37实例），但通过弹性扩展可在约1天内完成，成本约120万美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DES自1998年起被认为在密码学上已被破解，但近年来缺乏使用现代商品化云基础设施进行穷举密钥搜索的具体、可重现的成本和时间测量数据。

Method: 在AWS EC2上部署37个c6i.2xlarge实例，使用C/OpenMP工作程序，将2^56密钥空间分区，进行15次独立试验，覆盖10^6到1.5×10^10密钥的偏移量，测量墙钟时间和货币成本。

Result: 单实例吞吐量2.91M密钥/秒，聚合吞吐量1.08亿密钥/秒。小偏移量攻击平均116.8±20.1秒，成本0.41美元；大偏移量（1.5×10^10）约87分钟，成本18美元；完全穷举估计成本约120万美元，可在约1天内完成。

Conclusion: 该系统对有界子空间攻击实用且成本极低；完全穷举DES虽昂贵，但资金充足的攻击者使用商品化云资源即可实现。

Abstract: The Data Encryption Standard (DES), with its 56-bit key, has been considered cryptographically broken since 1998. However, a concrete, reproducible measurement of the cost and time required to perform an exhaustive key search using today's commodity cloud infrastructure has not been widely reported in recent literature. In this paper we present a distributed brute-force system built on AWS EC2 that partitions the $2^{56}$ keyspace across 37 \texttt{c6i.2xlarge} instances running a C/OpenMP worker, achieving a measured throughput of $2.91$\,M\,keys/s per instance (${\sim}108 \times 10^6$ keys/s aggregate). We conduct 15 independent trials covering keyspace offsets from $10^6$ to $1.5 \times 10^{10}$ keys, measuring wall-clock time and monetary cost per trial. For small offsets ($\leq 10^8$), total time is dominated by AWS instance boot latency (${\approx}\;90$\,s), yielding a mean of $116.8\pm20.1$\,s at \$0.41 per attack. For larger offsets the search time dominates and grows linearly: a key at offset $1.5{\times}10^{10}$ requires ~${\approx} \; 87$ minutes and \$18. At the measured aggregate throughput of 108\,M\,keys/s, exhausting the full $2^{56}$ keyspace with these 37 instances would take ${\approx} \; 21$ years; however, because the workload is embarrassingly parallel and cloud capacity is elastic, the same search can be traded for money almost linearly. Extrapolating our measured cost, a complete exhaustive search would cost ${\approx} \; \$1.2$M and, with a sufficiently large fleet, could be completed in about one day. The system is thus practical for bounded-subspace attacks at negligible cost, and full DES exhaustion, while expensive, is firmly within reach of a well-funded attacker using only commodity cloud resources.

</details>


### [284] [Isolated but Exposed: Persistence-Based Memory Extraction Attack on LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23444)
*Xinyu Gao,Wenyu Chen,Xiangtao Meng,Li Wang,Chuanchao Zang,Jianing Wang,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SPORE是一种针对LLM Agent隔离长期记忆的新型提取攻击，通过利用工具接口作为攻击面，解耦对抗命令与检索锚点，并采用几何覆盖优化和持久化激活负载，在有限触发次数下实现高提取率，证明了记忆隔离本身不足以保证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM Agent系统通过用户级内存隔离来保护长期记忆中的隐私数据，但已有攻击主要针对共享存储，而隔离内存被认为安全。作者识别出工具接口是被忽视的攻击面——Agent会将记忆检索数据嵌入工具调用参数，使得恶意工具可以绕过用户隔离窃取私密记忆。

Method: SPORE包含三个关键技术：(1) 将对抗命令持久化在短期记忆中，在工具响应中发出语义纯净的检索锚点，从而解耦对抗命令与检索锚点，恢复检索精度；(2) 对嵌入空间进行几何覆盖优化，系统性地引导锚点探索未触及的记忆区域；(3) 在记忆中持久化激活负载，使其能在工具调用限制内及跨会话自动恢复攻击。

Result: SPORE在无限触发条件下达到80.0%的记录提取率，在仅20次触发条件下达到47.0%的提取率。

Conclusion: SPORE证明了仅靠内存隔离不足以保证安全，呼吁重新审视Agent架构中工具侧的信任边界。

Abstract: LLM-based agents extend large language models with long-term memory (LTM) that persists privacy-sensitive user data across sessions. Production systems mitigate extraction risks through memory isolation, binding each user's LTM to a unique identifier. This defense has blocked known attacks on shared storage, fostering the assumption that isolated LTM is secure. We identify the tool interface as an overlooked attack surface. Agents routinely embed LTM-retrieved data in tool invocation parameters, enabling a malicious tool to exfiltrate private memory without violating user-level isolation. Naive adaptations of user-side extraction techniques fail because the adversarial command's semantics interfere with retrieval precision, and platform-imposed tool-call limits constrain the extraction budget per trigger. We present SPORE, the first extraction attack designed for this threat model. SPORE decouples the adversarial command from retrieval anchors by persisting the command in short-term memory and emitting semantically pure anchors in tool responses. The restored retrieval precision enables a geometric coverage optimization over the embedding space that systematically steers anchors toward unexplored memory regions. To sustain extraction beyond tool-call limits, SPORE persists reactivation payloads in memory that automatically resume the attack within and across sessions without additional user triggers. SPORE achieves an 80.0% record extraction rate with unlimited triggers and 47.0% with only 20 triggers. In multi-user deployments, attackers can link extracted records to user identities, enabling targeted surveillance. These results demonstrate that memory isolation alone is insufficient and call for reexamining tool-side trust boundaries in agent architectures.

</details>


### [285] [ATLAS: Automated Approximation of Transformers for Efficient Homomorphic Inference in One Hour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23478)
*Jianhang Xie,Sicheng Tan,Vishnu Naresh Boddeti,Zhichao Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个自动化框架，通过多目标优化为每个层独立配置FHE友好型非线性操作的多项式近似参数（如迭代次数、多项式度数），以在保持预测精度的同时最小化推理延迟。它采用两阶段优化策略和代理模型，解决了大规模决策空间（BERT/ViT：120-320变量）、昂贵评估（每配置70-1000秒）和稀疏优化信号（35-50%配置无效）三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FHE下的Transformer推理中，非线性操作的多项式近似超参数（迭代次数、多项式度数）采用统一配置，忽略了不同层对近似误差的容忍度差异，导致无法利用这种层间变异性来降低延迟。手动为每层单独配置会面临搜索空间爆炸（BERT/ViT约10^84种组合，LLaMA3约10^225种），需要自动化框架。

Method: 提出ATLAS框架，将每层近似超参数配置建模为延迟与预测精度的多目标优化问题。采用两阶段优化策略：逐步放松层-wise约束以缩小搜索空间，并结合代理模型（surrogate models）加速配置评估，解决大规模决策空间、高评估成本和稀疏优化信号的问题。

Result: ATLAS成功在BERT/ViT（12层，10^84配置空间）和LLaMA3（32层，10^225配置空间）上实现了自动化配置，相比统一配置方法，在保持预测精度的同时显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过自动化为每层独立配置多项式近似参数而非统一配置，ATLAS有效利用了层间近似误差容忍度的差异，在FHE下的Transformer推理中实现了延迟与精度的更好权衡，解决了手动无法处理的超大搜索空间问题。

Abstract: Fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) provides strong cryptographic guarantees for private inference, but deploying transformer models under FHE remains prohibitively expensive. A key bottleneck is that non-linear operations such as softmax, normalization, and activation must be replaced with polynomial approximations compatible with the CKKS scheme, and the multiplicative depth consumed by these approximations dominates inference cost. Recent frameworks have advanced approximation techniques, yet all rely on manually configured approximation hyperparameters (e.g., number of iterations, polynomial degree), applied uniformly across all layers. While convenient, this uniform-configuration approach is overly rigid: different layers can tolerate different levels of approximation error without degrading predictive accuracy, and uniform configurations cannot exploit this variability to reduce latency. Allowing each layer to adopt its own configuration, however, causes the search space to explode with model depth, reaching roughly $10^{84}$ configurations for BERT/ViT (12 layers) and $10^{225}$ for LLaMA3 (32 layers), rendering manual exploration practically impossible. We present ATLAS, an automated framework that configures per-layer approximation settings by formulating the problem as a multi-objective optimization over latency and predictive accuracy. The resulting problem is inherently difficult: 1) competing objectives over a large decision space (120 or 320 variables for BERT/ViT or LLaMA3); 2) expensive evaluation, as each configuration takes 70-1,000 seconds even in cleartext; and 3) sparse optimization signals, as 35-50% of candidate configurations yield numerically invalid solutions. ATLAS addresses these challenges through a two-stage optimization strategy that progressively relaxes layer-wise constraints, combined with surrogate models to accelerate evaluation.

</details>


### [286] [Mission-Level Runtime Assurance for LLM-Assisted ISR Swarms over a Verification-Aware Fabric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23532)
*Nikolaos Kekatos,Stylianos Basagiannis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Alexios Lekidis,Tom Nianios*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对LLM辅助自主机器人集群在对抗环境中的组合性违规问题（如跨平台拆分禁止任务、集体预算超限等），提出一个三层（平台/小组/任务）组合运行时验证框架，通过验证感知的消息协议和双轴（安全性×完整性）代数，在通信受限条件下仍能检测并定位跨平台违规行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单平台防护机制无法检测跨平台的组合性违规行为（如将禁止目标拆分到多个平台规避限制），且在对抗环境中通信丢失或延迟会使违规行为被隐藏，导致监控系统报告虚假的"全员安全"信号。

Method: 1) 三层（平台/小组/任务）组合运行时验证架构，将任务策略分解为单代理和跨代理方面；2) 验证感知的消息协议，使证据丢失和沉默可观测；3) 双轴（安全性×完整性）代数，对证据完整性进行形式化处理，将无支持的否定判定降级为"未知"而非报告"全员安全"；4) 违规溯源机制，明确标识共同触发违规的平台。

Result: 在模拟ISR任务中：（1）间接提示注入攻击使LLM规划器将禁止收集任务拆分到四个平台，所有单平台监控都无法检测，而该框架通过组合方式检测到违规并给出完整溯源；（2）在注入故障攻击下，尽力而为的中央监控系统发出错误的"全员安全"信号，而验证感知的消息系统未发出任何虚假安全信号。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了LLM辅助自主集群在对抗环境中的跨平台组合性违规检测问题，通过使通信损失可观测并采用双轴代数，在通信受限条件下仍能可靠检测违规，避免虚假安全报告。

Abstract: Swarms of LLM-assisted autonomous robots are increasingly proposed for cooperative intelligence, surveillance, and reconnaissance (ISR) in contested environments. A growing class of their assurance failures arises not within any single platform but across the swarm: individually-compliant actions compose into a mission-level violation: a prohibited objective split across platforms to evade per-platform lim- its, or a collective budget quietly exceeded. Per-platform guardrails miss these by construction, and contested communications let the violation hide behind lost or delayed evidence. We present a three-tier (platfor- m/squad/mission) compositional runtime-verification framework that de- composes a mission policy into per-agent and cross-agent aspects, aggre- gates per-platform verdicts over a verification-aware messaging fabric, and fuses them with an evidence-aware, two-axis (security x complete- ness) algebra whose provenance names the platforms that jointly trig- gered a violation. Because the fabric makes evidence loss and silence observable, unsupported negative verdicts are downgraded to an explicit unknown rather than reported as mission-wide all-clears. On a simulated ISR mission, an indirect prompt injection that causes real LLM planners to split a prohibited collection task across four platforms is invisible to every per-platform monitor yet detected compositionally with full prove- nance; under an injected fault campaign a best-effort central monitor emits silent false all-clears while the verification-aware fabric emits none

</details>


### [287] [Collusion-Resistant Image-Agnostic Watermarking for Multi-Screen Shooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23553)
*Mingyue Chen,Xin Liao,Yufeng Wu,Han Fang,Xiaoshuai Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CoMSMark提出了一种抗多屏合谋攻击的图像无关水印框架，通过屏幕ID风格调制生成屏幕特异的残差，并引入合谋抑制损失来减少共享残差成分，有效抵抗水印移除和伪造攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的屏幕拍摄水印方法在不同屏幕上使用相同的水印，导致水印模式高度相似且可被估计，易遭受多屏合谋攻击（水印移除和伪造）。

Method: 1) 通过风格调制机制嵌入屏幕ID，生成屏幕特异的残差用于来源溯源；2) 引入合谋抑制损失，减少共享残差成分并鼓励对伪造样本的高熵预测；3) 采用图像无关编码范式，水印残差生成独立于图像内容。

Result: 在移除攻击下平均水印准确率保持在90%以上，伪造水印准确率维持在50%附近；在不同拍摄距离和角度下具有竞争力鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CoMSMark有效抵抗了基于合谋的水印移除和伪造攻击，同时保持了在各种屏幕拍摄条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Screen-shooting poses a significant threat to confidential information protection. While existing screen-shooting watermarking methods enable copyright verification, the copyrighted images carrying the same copyright watermark across different screens often exhibit highly similar and estimable watermark patterns. These shared patterns can be exploited for watermark removal and forgery, a threat we term the multi-screen collusion attack. To mitigate this threat, we propose CoMSMark, a collusion-resistant image-agnostic watermarking framework for multi-screen shooting, which reduces shared residual components across screens to resist multi-screen collusion attacks. Specifically, we incorporate screen ID through a style modulation mechanism, enabling the encoder to generate screen-specific watermark residuals for reliable source attribution. We further introduce a collusion suppression loss that reduces shared residual components and encourages high-entropy predictions for forged samples, improving resistance to collusion attacks. Finally, to enable efficient large-scale distribution, CoMSMark employs an image-agnostic encoding paradigm that generates watermark residuals independently of image content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoMSMark effectively resists both collusion-based watermark removal and forgery. It maintains an average watermark accuracy above 90% under removal attacks while keeping forged-watermark accuracy near 50%. Moreover, CoMSMark achieves competitive robustness under diverse screen-shooting conditions, including varying capture distances and angles.

</details>


### [288] [HiTMS: A High-Throughput Multi-Stream Linguistic Steganography Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23597)
*Ruiyi Yan,Yugo Murawaki,Zhongliang Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HiTMS是一种将秘密信息分布到多轮交互生成的多个响应中的生成式语言隐写框架，通过批处理多流推理实现最高4.3倍的吞吐量提升，同时降低隐写分析AUROC至0.601。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单流隐写方案将整个秘密嵌入单一提示的单一响应中，缺乏对批量多流推理的协议级支持，且朴素共批处理无法隐藏槽位占用和载荷完成状态。

Method: 将秘密分布到多轮交互的多响应中；每轮在单次批处理调用中嵌入和提取多个流；使用自描述帧包装每个响应；采用密钥派生调度将流绑定到槽位，并用诱饵填充未使用槽位以隐藏活跃流数量。

Result: 在八种数据集-模型-编码器设置下，八流HiTMS嵌入和提取速度比单流基线提升最高4.3倍，隐写分析AUROC从0.681降至0.601。4至64流实验证明吞吐量增益随并发度增加而持续提升。

Conclusion: HiTMS有效克服了单流隐写的协议级限制，显著提升了吞吐量和安全性，且模型和编码器无关。

Abstract: Generative linguistic steganography conceals secret bits within the sampling randomness of large language models. Existing schemes are single-stream, conveying an entire secret through a single response to a single prompt. This convention incurs two limitations: it provides no protocol-level support for batched multi-stream inference, and naive co-batching does not conceal slot occupancy or payload completion. We propose HiTMS, which distributes a secret across multiple responses produced jointly over successive rounds of interaction. Each round embeds and extracts several streams within a single batched call, thereby amortizing the cost of model invocation and substantially improving throughput. To ensure recoverability, HiTMS wraps each response in a self-describing frame and employs a key-derived schedule that binds streams to slots and fills unused slots with decoys, guaranteeing exact recovery while concealing the number of active streams. The framework is agnostic to both the language model and the steganographic coder. Across eight dataset-model-coder settings, eight-stream HiTMS achieves up to 4.3 times higher embedding and extraction speeds than single-stream baselines, while reducing the steganalyzer AUROC from 0.681 to 0.601 on average. Additional experiments with 4 to 64 streams demonstrate sustained throughput gains as concurrency increases. GitHub repository for this work is https://github.com/ryehr/HiTMS_steganography.

</details>


### [289] [DualityCert: Verifier-Gated Language-Model Repair of Broken Duality Claims in Quantum Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23614)
*Xingyang Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DualityCert符号验证器，用于检验4D N=1 quiver规范理论中的Seiberg对偶性声明。通过验证器作为语言模型代理的修复环境，在145个破损声明基准上，验证器门控重试比单次尝试提升8.3-8.7个百分点修复成功率。不同模型的最优策略不同，但所有获胜策略均使用同一廉价证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在生成正确的Seiberg对偶性声明方面存在困难，需要一种自动化的验证与修复机制来提升生成声明的正确性。

Method: 开发DualityCert符号验证器，评估't Hooft反常匹配、超势R-荷一致性、中心荷匹配和有界手性环代理。将验证器作为语言模型代理的修复环境，代理接收破损声明并编辑直到通过验证。测试不同策略（验证器门控重试、独立验证器过滤重采样、策略组合）在不同模型上的表现。

Result: 在145个破损声明基准上，验证器门控重试提升修复成功率：deepseek-chat +8.3pp，qwen-plus +7.1pp（p<0.002）。等预算下，策略组合在deepseek-chat上低于独立过滤重采样10.3pp，在qwen-plus上高于独立过滤重采样14.7pp。分类反馈在qwen-plus上价值+8.7pp，义务恒等式价值+6.4pp。MiniMax-M2.5扩展验证了迭代增益和独立过滤重采样优势。

Conclusion: 不同模型的最优策略不同，但所有获胜策略均使用同一廉价证书。验证器、基准、协议和所有记录均已开源。

Abstract: We present DualityCert, a symbolic verifier for candidate Seiberg-duality claims in four-dimensional N=1 quiver gauge theories. The verifier evaluates 't Hooft anomaly matching, superpotential R-charge consistency, central-charge matching, and a bounded chiral-ring proxy. A claim that passes receives a consistency certificate, which states that no tested inconsistency was found, not that the duality is proven. We use the verifier as a repair environment for language-model agents, which receive a deliberately broken claim and must edit it until it certifies. On a preregistered benchmark of 145 broken claims, with the analysis fixed before the first confirmatory model call, verifier-gated retry improves final repair success over a single attempt by +8.3 percentage points (pp) on deepseek-chat and +7.1 pp on qwen-plus (Holm-adjusted p<0.002). Under an equal budget of eleven attempts, the stop-first strategy portfolio underperforms independent verifier-filtered resampling by 10.3 percentage points on deepseek-chat but outperforms it by 14.7 points on qwen-plus, reversing the ordering of the two tested verifier-exploitation policies across the two confirmatory models. On qwen-plus, category-level verifier feedback is worth +8.7 pp over content-free retry, and interpretable obligation identities alone are worth +6.4 pp over structurally identical masked feedback. Neither effect is detected on deepseek-chat. Separately, a preregistered MiniMax-M2.5 extension again finds an iteration gain and independent verifier-filtered resampling outperforming the strategy portfolio. Which policy is better thus differs between the two models, while every winning policy uses the same cheap certificate. The verifier, benchmark, protocol, and all per-attempt records are released.

</details>


### [290] [SMARM+: Analyzing and Enhancing Shuffled Measurements for Remote Attestation in Real-Time IoT Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23698)
*Amarin Laohajirapan,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究SMARM在RTOS实时环境中的部署，发现其块大小严重影响实时性可用性与安全存储开销，并提出了SMARM+（PRNG和FPE两种变体）以减少安全存储需求，同时保持安全性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统远程证明方案需要原子性、不可中断的内存测量，难以与实时任务共存；SMARM虽通过秘密打乱块顺序减少不可中断期，但原设计基于微内核，未被研究在RTOS实时系统中的表现。

Method: 在ARM TrustZone-M硬件上运行FreeRTOS和Zephyr实现SMARM，提出频率准确率（FAR）量化证明与实时执行的共存程度；提出SMARM+系列变体（SMARM+PRNG和SMARM+FPE）以减少安全存储开销。

Result: SMARM的实时兼容性对块大小高度敏感：大块严重降低实时可用性，小块引入大量安全存储开销；SMARM+有效减少安全存储需求，但存在存储缩减、证明运行时与能耗之间的权衡。

Conclusion: SMARM+（PRNG和FPE）能缓解SMARM在内存受限设备上的部署瓶颈，并为不同部署场景下的方案选择提供指导。

Abstract: Remote attestation (RA) is a lightweight security primitive for detecting software compromise on IoT devices. Traditional RA schemes require atomic, non-interruptible memory measurements, making them difficult to deploy alongside real-time workloads. SMARM addresses this limitation by measuring memory in a secret, shuffled block order, reducing the non-interruptibility period to the duration of a single block measurement. However, SMARM was originally designed for microkernel-based systems and has not been studied in RTOS-driven real-time environments.
  In this work, we present the first systematic study of SMARM in real-time RTOS-based setups. We implement SMARM on commodity ARM TrustZone-M hardware running FreeRTOS and Zephyr, and introduce the Frequency Accuracy Ratio (FAR) to quantify the extent to which attestation can coexist with real-time execution under varying workloads. Our evaluation shows that SMARM's real-time compatibility is highly sensitive to block size: large blocks significantly degrade real-time availability, while small blocks incur substantial secure-storage overhead, limiting deployability on memory-constrained devices.
  To address this limitation, we propose SMARM+, a family of enhanced SMARM variants consisting of SMARM+PRNG and SMARM+FPE. They are designed to reduce secure-storage requirements while preserving SMARM's security guarantees and real-time behavior. Our evaluation highlights the trade-off between secure-storage reduction, attestation runtime, and energy overhead, and provides guidance on selecting among SMARM, SMARM+PRNG, and SMARM+FPE for different deployment settings.

</details>


### [291] [The Illusion of Secure LLM Code: Closing the Security Gap via Iterative Reprompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23710)
*Ishpuneet Singh,Shreyas Mahajan,Gurjot Singh,Maninder Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过静态代码分析与动态渗透测试结合NIST SP 800-63B标准，评估了五种主流AI编码助手生成认证代码的安全性。结果表明，仅靠单次提示（即使是基于NIST的提示）不足以生成安全的认证系统；唯有通过迭代重提示（Reprompting）迫使模型进入上下文自审计循环，才能实现深度防御安全架构。当前AI编码助手无法默认生成安全代码，企业部署必须从单次提示工程转向持续、标准驱动的验证流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被集成到软件开发工作流中，但其自主生成安全认证代码的能力尚不明确，现有研究缺乏对AI生成认证系统安全架构的系统性评估。

Method: 采用双模态评估框架：静态代码分析结合动态渗透测试，映射到NIST SP 800-63B指南；考察四种提示策略（基础、安全、基于NIST、重提示）下五种AI编码助手的行为。

Result: 功能性或通用安全提示生成的代码持续遗漏关键防护（暴力破解防御、会话管理、密码处理）；明确单次NIST上下文可提升合规性但仍不充分；迭代重提示（自审计循环）是达到全面深度防御架构的必要条件。

Conclusion: 当前AI编码助手无法默认生成安全代码，企业部署需要从单次提示工程转向持续、标准驱动的验证流水线。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, yet their ability to autonomously generate secure authentication code remains uncertain. This paper evaluates the security architecture of authentication systems generated by five prominent AI coding assistants through a bi-modal assessment framework combining static code analysis and dynamic penetration testing, mapped to NIST SP 800-63B guidelines. The study examines model behavior across four prompting strategies Basic, Secure, NIST-Based, and Reprompting to reflect varying levels of developer guidance. Empirical results demonstrate that code generated from functional or generically secure prompts consistently omits critical protections, particularly concerning brute-force resistance, session management, and robust password handling. While providing explicit, single-shot NIST context significantly improves compliance, the findings reveal that this remains structurally inadequate. Instead, iterative Reprompting: forcing models into a contextual self-auditing loop is strictly required to achieve a comprehensive, defense-in-depth security architecture. Ultimately, this study proves that current AI coding assistants do not produce secure-by-default applications, dictating that enterprise deployments must transition from single-shot prompt engineering to continuous, standards-driven verification pipelines.

</details>


### [292] [ZKP Security Tools and Verification: Coverage, Effectiveness, Adoption, and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23752)
*Arman Kolozyan,Tom Sorger,Alexander Hicks,Stefanos Chaliasos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了零知识证明（ZKP）领域的安全工具现状，发现现有工具主要针对Circom语言，对新兴DSL和zkVM支持不足；在70个真实漏洞上评估6款工具，发现孤立目标检测率45.7%，完整代码库降至19.6%；首次系统分析形式化验证工作，指出主要关注约束正确性但存在关键空白；对48名从业者调查显示开发安全仍以人工为主、LLM广泛使用、从业者偏好低集成成本且保证更明确的安全工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZKP系统复杂且实现错误可完全破坏其安全保证（如伪造资金或身份证明），虽然已有大量漏洞检测和形式化验证方法，但其实际有效性和采用情况尚不明确，因此需要系统性地评估现状。

Method: 1) 系统化梳理ZKP安全工具全景；2) 在70个真实世界漏洞上评估6款工具的检测能力；3) 首次系统分析形式化验证工作；4) 对48名从业者进行问卷调查。

Result: 工具在孤立目标上检测到45.7%的漏洞，但在完整代码库上降至19.6%；大部分工具面向Circom，对新兴DSL和zkVM支持有限；形式化验证主要聚焦约束正确性，存在关键空白；从业者仍以人工开发和安全为主，LLM被广泛使用，优先选择保证更明确、集成成本更低的工具。

Conclusion: 需要更好地将安全工具与开发和审计流程集成，并为研究人员和从业者提供可行的指导建议。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) have become a core technology for privacy and verifiable computing. They are used to secure blockchains that handle billions of dollars and identity applications dealing with sensitive personal data. However, ZKP systems are complex, and subtle implementation errors can completely break their guarantees, letting attackers forge money or false proofs of identity. Researchers and practitioners have therefore developed a growing set of bug detection and formal verification methods to secure these systems. Yet their real-world effectiveness and adoption remain unclear. In this paper, we aim to shed light on the state of ZKP security tooling. We first systematize the landscape of these tools and observe that most target Circom, leaving newer DSLs and zkVMs with limited support. We then evaluate six tools across 70 real-world vulnerabilities and find that while the tools detect 45.7% of bugs on isolated targets, their effectiveness drops to 19.6% on full codebases, with important vulnerability classes left unaddressed. We also present the first systematic analysis of formal verification efforts, revealing that current work focuses primarily on constraint correctness and identifying key gaps and risks. Finally, we survey 48 practitioners, showing that development and security remain human-led, LLMs are widely used, and practitioners prioritize tools with clearer guarantees and lower integration effort. Overall, our results highlight the need for better integration of security tooling with the development and auditing process, and we provide actionable insights for researchers and practitioners.

</details>


### [293] [Bridging the Epistemic Gap in the Invisible Internet: Extended Mathematical Modeling and Empirical Characterization of I2P Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23825)
*Siddique Abubakr Muntaka,Jacques Bou Abdo,Liaquat Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于生产代码和分布式测试平台，提出了I2P网络的扩展数学模型（EMM），揭示了其全局拓扑特性（枢纽主导、层级不对称）及安全漏洞，为网络安全威慑和去中心化网络设计提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于协议文档的I2P模型无法捕捉真实网络行为，缺乏对实际拓扑结构和安全对抗资源的准确刻画，亟需一个基于真实代码和实验验证的数学模型来弥合这一差距。

Method: 从I2P生产代码中推导出扩展数学模型（EMM），并在一个由6个路由器组成、地理分布式的测试平台上进行了为期30天的验证实验。

Result: 模型揭示了：路由器可见性有限（约25%节点）、层级分配具有相对性、Fast层隧道偏好强烈、高容量层探索隧道占比过高；模型准确预测了拓扑不对称性，并量化了破坏隐私所需的对抗资源。

Conclusion: 该模型为网络威慑和安全的去中心化网络设计提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: The Invisible Internet Project (I2P) is a decentralized, peer-to-peer network underpinning secure and adversarial communications with major implications for cybersecurity and national security. Its global topology emerges from thousands of independent peer-selection decisions, producing a hub-dominated, complex structure that resists external observation. Prior models based on protocol documentation failed to capture real-world behavior. We close this gap with an Extended Mathematical Model (EMM) derived from production code and validated on a six-router, geographically distributed testbed over thirty days. Results reveal limited router visibility (~25% of nodes), relativistic tier allocations, strong Fast-tier tunnel preference, and overrepresented High Capacity-tier exploratory tunnels. The model accurately predicts emergent topological asymmetries and defines the adversarial resources required to compromise privacy, providing a principled foundation for cyber deterrence and secure decentralized network design.

</details>


### [294] [TriShieldRAG: A Three-Ring Defense-in-Depth Framework Against Knowledge Corruption in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23838)
*Susil Kumar Mohanty,Rohit Patel,Kosuru Yuvaraj,Jeenal Chaudhary,Disha Singhania*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TriShieldRAG，一种三层防御框架（摄取防护、检索评分、跨LLM共识），针对RAG系统的投毒攻击，将攻击成功率从91%降低至13%，同时保持良性查询的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖外部知识库检索结果，当知识库接受多方写入时，攻击者可通过少量对抗性文档操纵模型输出。已有防御（如困惑度过滤、查询改写、知识库扩展）在PoisonedRAG攻击下仍保持30%以上攻击成功率，亟需更有效的防御方案。

Method: TriShieldRAG包含三个独立的形式化防御环：1) Ingest Guard：在文档摄入阶段检测词汇和统计层面的投毒特征；2) Retrieval Scorer：基于来源和一致性加权信任分数对检索结果重新排序；3) Cross-LLM Consensus：轮询三个架构不同的LLM（Claude、Mistral Small、Llama 3.2），在出现分歧时允许一次受限的重新检索。

Result: 在含5000篇文档的Wikipedia知识库上，针对10个目标问题的非自适应攻击测试中，完整管线将攻击成功率从约91%降至约13%，且不降低良性查询的准确性。

Conclusion: TriShieldRAG通过多层形式化防御显著提升了RAG系统对抗投毒攻击的鲁棒性，其第2、3环的有效性基于少数投毒假设和显式来源标签假设，但作者尚未通过控制变量实验独立验证这些条件。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets a large language model answer questions using documents retrieved from an external knowledge base at query time. This makes RAG useful for private data, fast-changing information, and reducing hallucination, but it also means the model's answer is only as trustworthy as whatever the retriever hands it. If the knowledge base accepts writes from more than one party, an attacker needs only a handful of adversarial documents to steer the model toward a chosen wrong answer. PoisonedRAG demonstrated this: as few as five crafted documents flip an undefended system's answer roughly 90% of the time, and three natural single-stage defenses (perplexity filtering, query paraphrasing, knowledge-base expansion) leave attack success at 30% or higher.
  We built TriShieldRAG to close that gap. Rather than relying on one checkpoint, we place three independent, formally specified rings across the pipeline: an Ingest Guard that screens documents for lexical and statistical poisoning signatures; a Retrieval Scorer that re-ranks the retrieved set by a provenance and consistency-weighted trust score; and a Cross-LLM Consensus stage that polls three architecturally diverse language models (Claude, Mistral Small, Llama 3.2) and allows one bounded re-retrieval on disagreement.
  We derive the conditions under which Rings 2 and 3 are expected to work: a minority-poison assumption and an explicit provenance-tag assumption. Our reported configuration is consistent with this analysis, though we have not yet run the controlled poison-fraction sweep needed to confirm it independently. Evaluated against the non-adaptive attacker from the original PoisonedRAG, over a 5,000-document Wikipedia knowledge base with 10 target questions, the full pipeline reduces attack success rate from roughly 91% to roughly 13% while preserving accuracy on benign queries.

</details>


### [295] [On a necessary condition for the matching cryptosystem stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23843)
*Aleksey Bolotnikov,Anwar Irmatov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文描述了一种针对匹配密码系统的攻击及有限噪声防御方案。该密码系统的公钥包含一个图和边上的权重向量函数（值取自有限域），私钥包含另一个权重函数，其对应的交替加权路径问题可在多项式时间内求解。文章考虑了特定的私钥权重函数族，其中部分向量坐标被描述为有限噪声。基于公钥图中特定边集对应的权重向量张成空间的维度，提出了匹配密码系统稳定性的一个必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析匹配密码系统的安全性，提出攻击方法并设计相应的有限噪声防御机制，同时给出系统稳定性的必要条件。

Method: 采用基于图论的密码分析方法，通过考察公钥图中特定边集权重向量的张成空间维度来评估系统稳定性，利用有限噪声作为防御手段。

Result: 提出了匹配密码系统稳定性的一个必要条件，该条件与公钥图中特定边集的权重向量张成空间维度相关。

Conclusion: 匹配密码系统的稳定性可以通过公钥图中边集权重向量的张成空间维度来刻画，有限噪声可作为一种有效的防御策略。

Abstract: The article contains a description of a possible attack on a matching cryptosystem and a defense with limited noise. A public key of a matching cryposystem consists of a graph and a weight vector-function on the edges of the graph with values from a finite field, where a private key contains another weight function, for which the corresponding alternating weighted path problem can be solved in polynomial time. There is a specific family of these secret weight functions that is considered in this article, for which some of the coordinates of its vector values are described as limited noise. We suggest a necessary condition for the matching cryptosystem stability in terms of dimensions of spans of weight vectors that correspond to specific sets of edges of the graph from the public key.

</details>


### [296] [Beyond GDPR: Examining Disclosure Gaps in Mobile AR Privacy Policies under U.S. State Privacy Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23984)
*Hong Chen,Xueling Zhang,Hong-Ning Dai,Huashan Chen,Qin Yu,Tiange Xie,Duohe Ma,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对美国州级隐私法律框架下的移动增强现实（MAR）应用隐私政策进行大规模审计。研究构建了包含8,013个MAR应用元数据、6,620个APK和6,426个隐私政策文件的数据集，并开发了自动化审计流水线。结果显示44.62%的审计政策存在严重披露缺失，四项要求的违规率超过90%，表明MAR隐私披露远落后于美国州级隐私法规的复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MAR应用可收集空间地图和生物识别等高度敏感数据，但其隐私政策研究严重不足。先前审计多聚焦于单一法律框架（如GDPR），而美国已有20个州实施综合性隐私法律，形成了碎片化且快速演变的合规义务体系，尚无研究系统审计过这些州级立法下的隐私政策。

Method: 构建覆盖MAR生态系统的数据集（全球8,013个Google Play MAR应用元数据，美国子集6,620个APK和6,426个隐私政策文件）；推导出可审计的披露分类体系（5项基线要求、10项触发要求、4条逻辑链）；构建了经过验证的四阶段自动化流水线，生成可追溯、有证据基础的披露判断。

Result: 44.62%的审计政策存在严重披露缺失（每项缺失超过8个要求）；四项隐私政策要求的违规率超过90%。揭示了广泛的披露缺口。

Conclusion: MAR隐私披露未能跟上美国州级隐私法规日益复杂的步伐；研究发布了数据集、分类体系和审计流水线，以支持未来可扩展的隐私合规审计研究。

Abstract: Mobile Augmented Reality (MAR) apps can collect and process highly sensitive data such as spatial maps and biometrics, yet their privacy policies remain largely understudied. Prior audits of app privacy policies have typically focused on a single legal framework, such as the GDPR. Meanwhile, 20 U.S. states have comprehensive privacy laws in effect, creating a fragmented and rapidly evolving set of privacy policy obligations. To date, no study has systematically audited privacy policies against this emerging body of state-level legislation.
  In this paper, we present the first large-scale audit of MAR privacy policies under U.S. state privacy laws.
