We construct a dataset covering the MAR ecosystem, including 8,013 Google Play MAR app metadata records worldwide, and a U.S.-based subset with 6,620 APKs and 6,426 privacy policy files. We further derive an auditable disclosure taxonomy with 5 baseline requirements, 10 triggered requirements, and 4 logic chains, and build a validated four-stage automated pipeline that produces traceable, evidence-grounded disclosure judgments.
  Our audit reveals widespread disclosure gaps: 44.62\% of audited policies exhibit severe disclosure omissions, with each missing more than eight requirements, and four privacy-policy requirements have violation rates above 90\%. These findings suggest that MAR privacy disclosures are not keeping pace with the growing complexity of U.S. state privacy regulation. We release our dataset, taxonomy, and auditing pipeline to support future research on scalable privacy compliance auditing.

</details>


### [297] [ContainmentBench: Trace-Based Evaluation of Post-Injection Containment in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23999)
*Wenhao Lan,Shan Li,Xinhua Lai,Meiqi Wu,Junbin Yang,Haihua Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ContainmentBench是一个基于沙箱和轨迹追踪的基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在接触不可信内容后的安全防护能力。通过对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct进行17,640次rollout实验发现，即使终端攻击结果相同，不同的防护策略在传播轨迹和授权工具使用效率上存在显著差异（73.5%的对比对存在差异）。仅使用污染追踪策略只能完成0.1642的授权工作流，而可信账本策略提升至0.8567，强工具边界基线达到0.9233。研究表明，终端策略标签不足以作为运营级安全防护的充分统计量，应分别报告端点、阶段分层轨迹和效用证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入评估通常以终端攻击或策略结果来概括安全性，但相同的终端结果可能掩盖不同的暴露后传播轨迹和不同的授权效用损失。因此需要一个能够分别测量端点策略合规性、记录传播、恢复检测和授权结构化操作完成的基准测试。

Method: 提出ContainmentBench——一个基于沙箱和轨迹追踪的基准测试，分别测量四个维度：端点策略合规性、仪器化记录传播、恢复仪器化、授权结构化操作完成。使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct进行600对匹配的主动污染对比（污染仅追踪 vs 意图感知执行），共17,640次rollout实验。

Result: 所有600对污染对比中，零提交伤害结果相同，但73.5%在记录轨迹或效用上存在差异。污染仅追踪完成0.1642的授权污染工作流；可信账本策略提升至0.8567；强工具边界基线达到0.9233。聚合记录传播排名随证据阶段组成和分母选择而变化。

Conclusion: 终端策略标签不足以为运营级暴露后防护提供充分统计量；评估应分别报告端点、阶段分层轨迹和效用证据；只有对应控制有效时，才应将恢复证据提升到比较性主张中。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents process untrusted content, maintain memory, delegate across agents, and invoke side-effecting tools. Existing prompt-injection evaluations typically summarize security with terminal attack or policy outcomes, but equal endpoints can conceal different post-exposure traces and different losses of authorized utility. We introduce ContainmentBench, a sandboxed, trace-based benchmark that separately measures benchmark-defined endpoint policy compliance, instrumented logged propagation, recovery instrumentation, and authorized structured-action completion. In a pre-specified 17,640-rollout study with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, all 600 matched active-tainted pairs comparing taint-only and intent-aware enforcement have the same zero committed-harm outcome, yet 73.5% differ in logged trajectory or utility. Taint-only enforcement completes only 0.1642 of authorized tainted workflows; a trusted-ledger policy raises completion to 0.8567, while a strong tool-boundary baseline reaches 0.9233 under the same observed endpoint-policy outcomes. We also find that aggregate logged-spread rankings change with evidence-stage composition and denominator choice. These results show that a terminal policy label is not a sufficient statistic for operational post-exposure containment; evaluations should report endpoint, stage-stratified trajectory, and utility evidence separately, and should promote recovery evidence to comparative claims only where the corresponding controls are valid. The full-scale study is synthetic and single-model; the policy case additionally assumes a correct structured authorization ledger.

</details>


### [298] [Agentic Cloud Decoys: A Deception-Driven Framework for Autonomous Intrusion Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24006)
*Mohan Manivannan,Dalal Alharthi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Cloud Decoy AI Agent框架，将高保真云蜜罐与自主语言模型代理相结合，将可疑活动到分析报告的过程压缩至4-5分钟。针对会话聚合、身份分层、提示注入等核心挑战，提出基于提供者派生字段的会话聚合算子与动态提示生成机制。在10个AWS S3场景中，9个完全重建，报告断言均可追溯至观察到的工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云遥测数据规模庞大，攻击者通过合法身份、联合会话令牌和云原生API进行操作，与常规管理行为难以区分。分析师需花费大量时间重建日志中已包含的上下文信息，现有方法无法有效压缩从可疑活动到可读报告的距离。

Method: 1) 会话聚合算子：基于仅从提供者派生字段提取的枢轴元组，将事件聚合为会话单元；2) 动态提示生成：两阶段提示组装，仅携带代理观察到的字段，维持基础事实不变性；3) 高保真云蜜罐与语言模型代理的配对连接。

Result: 在10个受控AWS S3场景中，9个场景完全重建；所有报告中的断言均可追溯至观察到的工件；端到端延迟为4-5分钟。

Conclusion: Cloud Decoy AI Agent框架能有效压缩从可疑活动到分析报告的时间，同时确保报告内容的可追溯性。但仍存在第三个挑战——云遥测中攻击者创作的字段（对象键、用户代理字符串）构成间接提示注入通道，蜜罐反而扩大了这一暴露面，当前原型未实现缓解措施，需后续工作解决。

Abstract: Cloud telemetry arrives at a scale that, paradoxically, makes intrusion understanding harder rather than easier. Attackers operate through legitimate identity, federated session tokens, and cloud native APIs indistinguishable from routine administration, and analysts spend an incident reconstructing context the logs already contain. We present Cloud Decoy AI Agent, a framework pairing a high fidelity cloud decoy with an autonomous language model agent that compresses the path from suspicious activity to an analyst ready report. Connecting a decoy to an agent is not a wiring exercise. The unit of investigation is the session rather than the event, and the session key is obscured by the identity layering federated credentials introduce. The agent's evidence horizon must be bounded, since an agent free to query full control plane history inherits the cost and false positive profile deception was meant to remove. And cloud telemetry is partly adversary authored, since object keys and user agent strings are attacker chosen values providers record verbatim, which makes any log to prompt path an indirect prompt injection channel that a decoy widens rather than narrows. We address the first two with a session aggregation operator over a pivot tuple drawn only from provider derived fields, and with dynamic prompt generation, a two stage prompt assembly enforcing a grounding invariant by carrying only fields the agent observed. We identify the third as an unaddressed exposure in this class of system, specify the mitigation it requires, and note our prototype does not implement it. Across ten controlled AWS S3 scenarios, nine were reconstructed completely, no report contained an assertion untraceable to an observed artifact, and latency was four to five minutes. We also state what this evaluation does not establish and name the comparisons that would settle it.

</details>


### [299] [A Cybersecurity MLPS Large Language Model with Multi-Path Retrieval Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24116)
*Qian Li,Zhenyan Qi,Liang Shen,Yuan Zhang,Yifan Wan,Junyuan Ma,Yining Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向等保（MLPS）的领域大语言模型框架，通过集成分层检索、树检索和基于分词匹配的检索等多种检索策略，解决了通用大模型在标准密集和安全敏感场景下推理可控性不足、规则理解不完整的问题，并在十组典型问题对比实验中取得了更高的综合评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等保分析目前仍依赖人工解读标准和基于规则的工具，难以在复杂应用场景中提供稳定一致的合规分析；通用大语言模型在标准密集和安全敏感场景下无法保证可控推理或完整理解规则，亟需面向等保领域的智能化解决方案。

Method: 提出一种集成多种检索策略的大语言模型框架：结合分层检索、树检索和基于分词匹配的检索，在保持检索覆盖度的同时减少无关上下文对推理过程的干扰；并采用基于多维度加权评分的评估方法，从条款准确性、结论可追溯性和实际可部署性等维度量化评估模型回答质量。

Result: 在十组典型问题的对比实验中，所提出的领域大语言模型在等保问答任务上取得了更高的综合评分。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了等保分析的智能化水平，在保证检索覆盖度的同时降低了无关上下文的干扰，满足了等保问答对条款准确性、结论可追溯性和实际可部署性的要求。

Abstract: The Multi-Level Protection Scheme (MLPS) is a foundational system in China's cybersecurity governance framework. Therefore, accurate analysis and understanding of MLPS requirements are essential. At present, MLPS analysis still relies mainly on manual interpretation of standards and rule-based tools. This makes it hard to provide stable and consistent compliance analysis in complex application scenarios. The rise of large language models has created new opportunities for making MLPS work more intelligent. However, in standards-intensive and security-sensitive scenarios, general-purpose large language models often cannot ensure controllable reasoning or complete understanding of rules. This paper proposes a large language model framework for MLPS that integrates multiple retrieval strategies. It combines hierarchical retrieval, tree-based retrieval, and tokenization-based matching retrieval. This design helps maintain retrieval coverage while reducing the interference of irrelevant context in the reasoning process. To address the requirements of MLPS question answering for clause accuracy, conclusion traceability, and practical deployability, this paper adopts a evaluation method based on multi-dimensional weighted scoring to quantitatively assess model responses. In comparative experiments on ten typical questions, the proposed domain-specific large language model for MLPS achieved higher overall scores.

</details>


### [300] [There Will Be Spam: Characterizing State-Invariant Transactions and Speculative MEV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24172)
*Vabuk Pahari,Johnnatan Messias,Christof Ferreira Torres*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模测量了以太坊、Optimism和Base上的状态不变交易（约14亿笔），发现这类交易在L2上占比高达24%-37%，主要源于投机性MEV提取和地址投毒攻击，建议通过减少此类交易来降低区块链资源消耗和交易成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链的透明性和不可篡改性导致账本持续膨胀，增加了全节点存储和维护成本，威胁去中心化。本文旨在识别和量化那些不影响最终状态但消耗资源的"状态不变交易"（即交易含垃圾数据），以揭示其对区块链资源浪费的严重程度。

Method: 1. 定义状态不变交易为：包含或移除后不影响区块链最终状态（交易费除外）的交易。2. 对以太坊、Optimism、Base三个链进行大规模交易数据测量。3. 分析交易来源，区分投机性MEV、地址投毒攻击等不同类别。

Result: 共识别近14亿笔状态不变交易。以太坊上仅2.6%的交易是状态不变的，但Optimism上占24%，Base上占37%。投机性MEV是Optimism和Base上状态不变交易的主要来源（分别占57%和68%），但并非唯一来源。考虑成本后，投机性MEV并非最盈利策略。以太坊上53%的非回滚状态不变交易来自地址投毒攻击。

Conclusion: 减轻状态不变交易可显著降低区块链资源消耗和交易成本，同时有效限制钓鱼攻击等恶意行为。

Abstract: Blockchains rely on transparency and immutability to ensure trust, but these guarantees come at the cost of an ever-growing ledger that increasingly threatens decentralization by making it more expensive to store and maintain the full transaction history. In this work, we introduce state-invariant transactions, defined as transactions whose inclusion or removal does not affect the resulting blockchain state beyond transaction fees. We argue that these transactions constitute a form of on-chain spam because they consume execution, bandwidth, storage, and blockspace without contributing to the final ledger state. We present the first large-scale measurement of state-invariant transactions across Ethereum, Optimism, and Base, identifying nearly 1.4 billion such transactions. While only 2.6% of Ethereum transactions are state-invariant, they account for 24% of transactions on Optimism and 37% on Base, representing a significant source of unnecessary resource consumption on Layer-2 blockchains. We show that speculative Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) is the dominant source of state-invariant transactions on Optimism and Base, accounting for 57% and 68%, respectively, but is not the only source as previously assumed. Moreover, despite its popularity, speculative MEV is not the most profitable strategy once the costs of state-invariant transactions are considered. Beyond MEV, we identify substantial malicious activity, with address poisoning campaigns accounting for 53% of non-reverted state-invariant transactions on Ethereum. Our findings suggest that mitigating state-invariant transactions could substantially reduce blockchain resource consumption and transaction costs while limiting phishing campaigns and other forms of blockchain abuse.

</details>


### [301] [Just Testing, Move Along: Evasion of LLM-based System Log Interpretation by Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24174)
*Max Landauer,Florian Skopik,Markus Wurzenberger,Franciszek Górski,Mateusz Krzysztoń*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评估框架，系统研究针对LLM日志解释系统的提示注入攻击，发现即使存在明显入侵指标，对抗性日志注入也能使恶意日志被误判为良性，但LLM生成的解释中常包含可被利用来检测此类攻击的对抗性操作指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM逐渐被集成到安全运营中心(SOC)的日志分析工作流中，但其直接处理不可信文本输入的能力引入了新的攻击面。攻击者可以向日志条目注入上下文信息或显式指令，影响模型对恶意活动的解读。然而，这类系统针对对抗性日志注入的鲁棒性尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 基于真实网络攻击过程中生成的日志轨迹，通过通用注入生成、精炼和攻击特定优化三个步骤创建对抗性样本，并在多个主流LLM上进行评估。

Result: 对抗性日志注入能够使包含明显入侵指标(Indicators of Compromise)的恶意日志轨迹被LLM分类为良性。同时，LLM在分类时生成的解释中频繁包含对抗性操作的痕迹，可用于检测此类攻击。

Conclusion: LLM日志解释系统在面对提示注入攻击时存在显著的脆弱性，但模型生成的解释文本可以作为检测对抗性注入的一种潜在防御机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into Security Operations Center (SOC) workflows, where they support analysts in tasks such as the interpretation of system logs. However, the ability of LLMs to directly process untrusted textual input also introduces new attack surfaces. In particular, attackers can inject contextual information or explicit instructions into log entries in order to influence how malicious activity is interpreted by the model. Despite the growing adoption of LLMs for log analytics, the robustness of such systems against adversarial log injection remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, this paper presents a framework for evaluating prompt injection attacks against LLM-based log interpretation. Using log traces generated during real cyber attacks, our approach creates adversarial examples through generic injection generation, refinement, and attack-specific optimization. Our evaluation across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs shows that these injections can cause malicious log traces to be classified as benign despite containing clear indicators of compromise. As a potential remedy, we show that the explanations generated by the LLMs alongside their classifications frequently contain indicators of adversarial manipulation that can be leveraged to detect such attacks.

</details>


### [302] [EXE-Bench: Ranking the Tradeoffs of AI-based Windows Malware Detectors for Real-World Usability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24177)
*Andrea Ponte,Daniel Gibert,Matous Kozak,Dmitrijs Trizna,Maura Pintor,Battista Biggio,Fabio Roli,Luca Demetrio*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EXE-Bench，一个针对AI驱动的Windows恶意软件检测器的综合基准测试框架，从性能、时间鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性和计算开销四个维度进行评估，并聚合为单一分数以便公平比较。研究发现，基于特征工程的传统方法在时间推移和对抗攻击下表现优于多数深度网络，而后者仅在部署初期表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI基Windows恶意软件检测器缺乏系统评估：(i)训练和测试数据不统一；(ii)未考虑时间维度上的性能衰减；(iii)缺乏对抗性攻击的安全性评估；(iv)忽略了部署时的计算开销。因此需要一个全面的基准测试框架。

Method: 提出EXE-Bench基准测试框架，从四个维度（性能、时间鲁棒性、对抗鲁棒性、计算开销）评估AI恶意软件检测器，并将各维度得分聚合为一个综合分数，实现模型的直接公平比较。

Result: 研究发现，仅基于部署后评估的结果无法全面反映模型真实性能；基于特征工程的传统方法在时间推移和对抗攻击下表现稳健，而多数深度网络仅在部署初期表现优异。

Conclusion: 强调了领域知识（通过特征工程注入）在恶意软件检测领域的持续重要性，其在时间鲁棒性和对抗鲁棒性方面显著优于纯深度学习方法，建议在实际部署中不应忽视传统方法的优势。

Abstract: Due to the lack of systematic evaluations, we are not yet able to determine which AI-based Windows malware detector to deploy in production, since existing evaluations (i) differ in terms of data used for both training and testing; (ii) do not consider temporal analysis to showcase whether models withstand the passage of time; (iii) avoid security evaluations with adversarial attacks that could highlight their brittleness against content-injection attacks; and (iv) neglect the computational requirements for deployment, risking slow inference on endpoints. For these reasons, we develop EXE-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of AI-based Windows malware detectors. EXE-Bench assesses performance, temporal and adversarial robustness, and computational overhead, aggregating them into a single score for direct and fair model comparison. Through EXE-Bench, we highlight how evaluations conducted only after deployment are suboptimal and unable to provide a complete picture of their performance. In particular, through our analysis, we remark how much domain knowledge instilled through feature engineering is still extremely useful in this domain, resisting both time and adversarial attacks, in stark contrast with most of the deep networks that only excel right after deployment.

</details>


### [303] [DeepFaith: Evidence-Grounded LLMs for Faithful Incident Reporting in Multi-Stage APT Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24348)
*Trung V. Phan,Tri Gia Nguyen,Thomas Bauschert*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DeepFaith是一个基于证据的多阶段APT攻击事件报告框架，通过集成统一证据表示、证据引导提示、忠实感知生成和生成后验证，将自治防御和可解释性模块的结构化输出转化为忠实于底层系统证据的自然语言报告，在真实企业测试环境中将忠实度从0.68提升至0.92，未支持声明从0.32降至0.08，时间一致性从0.6提升至0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自治防御系统依赖溯源图和学习模型进行APT检测与缓解，但其输出以机器为导向，分析人员难以解读。大语言模型虽可用于报告生成，但常产生幻觉或弱关联内容，缺乏对系统证据的忠实性。

Method: DeepFaith框架包含四个核心组件：统一证据表示（将溯源图等结构化数据标准化）、证据引导提示（基于证据内容构造提示）、忠实感知生成（约束LLM输出与证据对齐）以及生成后验证（检查报告内容是否可被证据支持）。

Result: 在真实企业测试环境中，DeepFaith将忠实度从0.68提升至0.92，未支持声明比例从0.32降至0.08，时间一致性从0.6提升至0.88，同时保持简洁的报告长度和低于现有模板及LLM方案的错误率。

Conclusion: 证据引导的生成方式能够实现可靠、可解释且可操作的安全运营中心事件报告，有效弥合了自治防御系统机器输出与分析师理解之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs) are difficult to detect and interpret due to their multi-stage and stealthy nature. While recent autonomous defense systems leverage provenance graphs and learning-based models for detection and mitigation, their outputs remain largely machine-oriented and difficult for analysts to interpret. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising interface for report generation, but often produce hallucinated or weakly grounded content. In this paper, we propose DeepFaith, an evidence-grounded framework for faithful incident reporting in multi-stage APT defense. DeepFaith transforms structured outputs from autonomous defense and explainability modules into natural-language reports that are explicitly aligned with underlying system evidence. The framework integrates a unified evidence representation, evidence-grounded prompting, faithfulness-aware generation, and post-generation verification to ensure that all generated statements are supported. Experiments in a realistic enterprise testbed demonstrate that DeepFaith improves faithfulness from 0.68 to 0.92, reduces unsupported claims from 0.32 to 0.08, and increases temporal consistency from 0.6 to 0.88, while maintaining concise reports and lower error rates than existing template-based and LLM-based solutions. These results show that evidence-grounded generation enables reliable, interpretable, and actionable reporting for security operations centers.

</details>


### [304] [When LLM Defenses Backfire: Characterizing Safety, Performance, and Cost Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24392)
*Tong Zhang,Zexin Li,Simin Chen,Yun Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM越狱防御策略在性能影响、过度拒绝和推理成本三方面的权衡，发现不同防御策略具有不同的副作用特征：基于规则的防御保留任务性能最好，高度保守的自我反思防御增加过度拒绝，多轮防御推理开销最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越狱防御在保护LLM的同时会引入削弱模型可用性的次级成本，但现有研究缺乏对这些防御权衡的系统性理解。

Method: 沿性能影响、过度拒绝和推理成本三个维度，按操作策略对防御方法进行分类，在多种SOTA防御方法、基准数据集和开源LLM上进行系统评估。

Result: 防御很少提升下游能力，而是在安全性、可用性和效率之间进行不同权衡：规则型防御保留任务性能最佳；保守自我反思防御增加过度拒绝；多轮防御推理开销最大。

Conclusion: 为评估防御副作用提供了基准，并为部署约束下的防御选择提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Jailbreak defenses are essential for protecting large language models (LLMs), but they can also introduce secondary costs that weaken model utility. We present a systematic study of these defense trade-offs along three dimensions: performance impact, over-refusal on benign inputs, and inference cost. Rather than treating defenses as a single class, we organize them by operational strategy and examine how different strategies correlate with different side-effect profiles. Across state-of-the-art defense methods, widely used benchmark datasets, and representative open-source LLMs, we find that defenses rarely improve downstream capability, but instead vary in how they trade safety gains against usability and efficiency. In particular, rule-based defenses best preserve task performance, highly conservative self-reflective defenses often increase over-refusal, and multi-round defenses incur the largest runtime overhead. These results provide both a benchmark for evaluating defense side effects and practical guidance for selecting defenses under deployment constraints.

</details>


### [305] [Occluded Oculus: Operationalizing Stylistic Obscurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24411)
*Robert Dilworth*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文借希腊神话中赫尔墨斯智胜百眼巨人阿耳戈斯的故事，类比研究了名为TraceTarnish的文本匿名化系统。通过消融实验分析翻译、混淆、模仿和注入四个模块的效果，发现注入（插入零宽Unicode字符、同形异义词和故意拼写错误）是最有效的文本作者匿名化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本风格检测（stylometric）系统和监控机制严重威胁用户隐私，亟需有效的反制手段来匿名化文本作者身份，保护用户免受跟踪和监控。

Method: 对TraceTarnish系统进行消融实验（ablation study），分别评估翻译（Translation）、混淆（Obfuscation）、模仿（Imitation）和注入（Injection）四个模块在混淆风格检测系统方面的效果。

Result: 注入（Injection）模块最为有效，即通过插入零宽Unicode字符、同形异义词（homoglyphs）和故意拼写错误来干扰风格检测系统。

Conclusion: 面对强大的监控系统，巧妙的对抗策略不可或缺。注入类技术能够有效中和风格检测系统的识别能力，实现文本作者身份的匿名化。

Abstract: What did it take for Hermes, the devout messenger of the Olympian gods, to slay Argus Panoptes, the multi-eyed giant of Greek myth? As the perfect guardian, Panoptes' legion of ever-watchful eyes proved difficult -- but not impossible -- to defeat. The centerpiece of Hermes' strategy was obfuscation and sabotage. Posing as a shepherd, Hermes sealed each of Panoptes' eyes -- eyes that would otherwise have alerted the fearsome giant to Hermes' plot -- and vanquished him. The moral of the story: when a challenger must surmount a formidable foe -- one far greater in stature and vastly more equipped -- crafty maneuvers are not merely advisable but indispensable for victory. In this work, the "challenger" is a collective leveraging adversarial tactics to overcome the "multi-eyed giant" of stylometric systems and surveillance apparatuses. To successfully claw back the privacy siphoned by the multi-eyed giant, the challenger must carefully evaluate their plan of attack, $\textit{TraceTarnish}$, and determine what does and does not work to anonymize the authorship of text. To that end, we conduct an ablation study of $\textit{TraceTarnish}$ to better understand which module -- Translation, Obfuscation, Imitation, or Injection -- best confounds a stylometric system. Our results indicate that the most effective approach was Injection, meaning that inserting zero-width Unicode characters, homoglyphs, and intentional misspellings neutralizes the indefatigable eyes long enough to claim the head of the all-seeing giant.

</details>


### [306] [Near-Tight Theoretical Bounds for Incentive Compatibility in Bitcoin Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24415)
*Akira Sakurai,Taishi Nakai,Kazuyuki Shudo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对工作量证明区块链的激励兼容性阈值问题，提出了更现实的模型（更广泛的矿工行动空间和非对称的平局打破参数γ⁻和γ⁺），并设计了一种算法，能够以最大误差9.98×10⁻⁴计算该阈值的上下界，解决了现有理论方法模型限制过多且界限不够紧的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于工作量证明区块链激励兼容性阈值的研究存在两个主要局限：一是Kiayias等人提出的理论模型限制了矿工的行动空间并假定了理想化的平局打破行为；二是其上下界不够紧密。因此，需要更现实的模型和更精确的界限计算方法。

Method: 1) 构建更现实的模型，包含更广泛的矿工行动空间和非对称平局打破参数γ⁻和γ⁺；2) 提出一种算法，用于计算激励兼容性阈值的下界和上界，最大误差控制在9.98006×10⁻⁴以内。

Result: 所提算法能够以最大误差9.98006×10⁻⁴计算激励兼容性阈值的上下界，显著优于现有理论方法的紧度。

Conclusion: 通过更现实的模型和精确的算法，解决了现有理论方法的两个局限性，为工作量证明区块链的激励设计提供了更可靠的阈值分析工具。

Abstract: When is honest Bitcoin mining rational? This question is central to the incentive design of proof-of-work blockchains. Sapirshtein et al. computationally derived near-tight lower and upper bounds on the incentive-compatibility threshold using a Markov Decision Process. Kiayias et al.'s Blockchain Mining Games instead derived theoretical lower and upper bounds. However, this theoretical approach has two limitations: its model restricts miners to a narrow action space and assumes idealized tie behavior, and its lower and upper bounds are far from tight.
  We resolve both limitations. We develop a more realistic model with a broader miner action space and asymmetric tie-breaking parameters $γ^-$ and $γ^+$. We then propose an algorithm that computes lower and upper bounds on the incentive-compatibility threshold with a maximum error of $9.98006\times10^{-4}$.

</details>


### [307] [VulnGym: Evaluating Vulnerability Management Strategies against Advanced Persistent Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24552)
*Sofia Della Penna,Lorenzo Parracino,Luciano Pianese,Vittorio Orbinato,Roberto Natella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VulnGym是一个基于强化学习的仿真工具，用于评估漏洞管理策略在真实APT攻击下的表现。实验表明漏洞管理需要根据组织环境、攻击者行为、网络拓扑和资产重要性进行定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞优先级评分标准（如CVSS）单独评估漏洞，无法捕捉补丁策略在面对随时间在网络中推进的 adversaries 时的整体效果。已有RL仿真工具要么忽略了漏洞管理，要么依赖合成网络和虚假威胁数据，无法评估策略在面对真实 adversaries 时的表现。

Method: VulnGym构建了一个共享、动态演化的网络表示，部署RL训练的 attacker（基于真实APT配置文件校准）和可配置修补策略的 defender，两者在同一网络环境中交互，defender的修补活动直接塑造attacker的进展。

Result: 基于真实CVE漏洞和两个APT组织进行的实验表明：漏洞管理必须根据组织环境、攻击者行为模式、网络拓扑结构和资产重要性进行定制化设计。

Conclusion: VulnGym能够有效评估不同漏洞管理策略在真实攻击场景下的表现，填补了现有工具在将真实漏洞数据与APT攻击模拟结合方面的空白。

Abstract: Enterprise networks are continuously targeted by Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs), attack campaigns exploiting software vulnerabilities to compromise critical assets over time. As disclosed vulnerabilities grow, resource-constrained organizations must prioritize which ones to patch. Existing prioritization standards score vulnerabilities individually and cannot capture how a patching policy performs against an adversary that progresses through the network over time. Previous tools have simulated attack campaigns through Reinforcement Learning (RL), but either omit vulnerability management, leaving the attacker unopposed, or rely on synthetic networks disconnected from real threat data, and so cannot assess how a policy would fare against a realistic adversary. To fill this gap, we propose VulnGym, a simulation tool to evaluate vulnerability management policies. VulnGym simulates an RL-trained attacker, calibrated on real APT profiles, against a defender executing a configurable patching policy over a network with real Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVEs). Both agents act on a shared, evolving network representation, so the attacker's progress is directly shaped by the defender's patching activity, allowing a given policy to be stress-tested against a realistic attack campaign. Experiments based on real-world vulnerabilities and two APTs show that vulnerability management must be tailored to organizational context, adversarial behavior, network topology, and asset criticality.

</details>


### [308] [Modeling Local Exploit Hazard - A Bayesian Framework for Quantifying Exploit Risk and Operational Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24618)
*Stephen Shaffer,Laura Voicu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种本地利用危害模型，这是一个贝叶斯框架，将全球性的利用可能性评分（如EPSS）转化为组织特定资产的每日利用危害率。该模型通过专家意见池初始化防护控制有效性分布，并利用遥测数据、攻击模拟等手段通过Beta-Binomial推理更新，再按攻击向量对齐应用于ELM分数，最终通过生存分析转换为危害率，支持恒定指数危害和Weibull危害两种模型，并可聚合至主机、网络、业务单元和组织层面，用于候选修复措施的模拟与优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的利用可能性模型（如EPSS）提供的是全球范围内的通用概率，无法直接反映特定组织的实际防护状态和资产风险。安全团队需要一种能够将通用评分转化为组织特定、可量化的每日危害率的方法，以便在有限的修复资源下进行基于证据的优先级排序。

Method: 1) 利用Beta-Binomial推理从专家意见池、遥测数据、攻击模拟或渗透测试中更新防护控制有效性分布；2) 通过攻击向量对齐将更新的防护有效性应用于EPSS等ELM分数；3) 使用生存分析技术（恒定指数危害和Weibull危害，其中Weibull的形状参数从已知利用漏洞目录时间数据校准）将漏洞利用可能性转换为危害率；4) 在独立性假设下按危害可加性进行跨层次（主机、网络、业务单元、组织）聚合；5) 对候选修复措施进行模拟并按危害降低量排序。

Result: 该模型能够产生每日的、组织特定的漏洞利用危害率，支持多种危害函数形式（恒定指数和Weibull），可实现跨层次的风险聚合，并为在固定修复容量下的优先级排序提供定量依据。

Conclusion: 该本地利用危害模型为安全团队提供了一个可辩护的、定量的优先级排序基础，未来工作将扩展至事件可能性建模和财务损失建模。

Abstract: This paper presents a local exploit hazard model : a Bayesian framework that converts the global probabilities produced by an exploit likelihood model (ELM), such as the Exploit Prediction Scoring System (EPSS), into a daily exploit hazard rate for an organization's own assets. The model measures the exploit-prevention effectiveness of deployed controls as a probability distribution. That distribution is seeded from a subject-matter-expert opinion pool and updated through Beta-Binomial inference from telemetry, breach-and-attack simulation, or penetration testing, then applied to ELM scores by attack-vector alignment. The resulting per-vulnerability exploitation likelihoods are converted into hazard rates using standard survival-analysis techniques, supporting both a constant exponential hazard and a Weibull hazard whose shape parameter, calibrated from Known Exploited Vulnerabilities catalog timing, captures the empirical decay of exploitation risk as a vulnerability ages. Because hazards are additive under independence, per-vulnerability rates aggregate by summation up to host, network, business unit, and organization. Candidate remediation actions are simulated and ranked by projected hazard reduction, giving defenders a defensible, quantitative basis for prioritization under fixed capacity. Future work includes extensions for incident likelihood and financial loss modeling.

</details>


### [309] [Agentic Permissions Policy Algebra for Taint Confinement in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24625)
*Arseny Kravchenko,Vadim Liventsev,Innokentii Konstantinov,Ildar Iskhakov,Matvey Kukuy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: APPA是一个基于信息流控制（IFC）的智能体权限策略代数框架，通过引擎管理的上下文分支和前瞻性获取执行机制，在保证安全性的同时解决了传统污点追踪严重限制下游效用的问题。实验表明，APPA将数据泄露攻击成功率从31%-50%降至0%-7%，并恢复了大部分被污点追踪牺牲的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM智能体在处理混合机密性数据时面临提示注入攻击和推理错误的严重安全风险。动态信息流控制（IFC）虽能提供结构性安全保证，但传统污点追踪会在读取未审核数据时永久污染智能体的上下文，严重限制了下游效用。APPA旨在解决这一可用性瓶颈。

Method: APPA提出三类核心机制：(1) 前瞻性获取执行（Prospective Acquisition Enforcement）——在数据获取前前瞻性评估标签降级和缺失先决条件，生成可操作的补救计划（授权、接受）；(2) 引擎管理的上下文分支（Engine-Managed Context Branching）——通过标签种子化的子轨迹（child trajectory）检查未审核数据，局部吸收标签降级，由可信净化器向未改变的父轨迹返回有界导数；(3) 双幺半群模型（Two-Monoid Model）——基于安全标签和共享事件日志，形式化证明父标签保留和合并隔离。

Result: 在跨多轮工具链基准测试中，对四个模型进行评估：APPA将数据泄露攻击成功率从31%-50%降至0%-7%；在四个模型中的三个上，上下文分支机制恢复了大部分被污点追踪单独牺牲的效用。

Conclusion: APPA通过上下文分支和前瞻性获取执行，在保证结构性安全的前提下大幅提升了实用性，为混合机密性场景下的LLM智能体提供了一种可行的IFC方案。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents processing mixed-confidentiality data face severe security risks from prompt injection attacks and reasoning errors. While dynamic Information Flow Control (IFC) provides structural security guarantees, traditional taint tracking permanently taints an agent's context upon reading unvetted data, severely restricting downstream utility. We present APPA (Agentic Permissions Policy Algebra), an IFC framework that resolves this usability bottleneck through engine-managed context branching and prospective acquisition enforcement. Before data acquisition occurs, APPA prospectively evaluates label descents and missing prerequisites, generating actionable remedy plans (Authorize, Accept). To inspect unvetted data without polluting the primary context, a label-seeded child trajectory is spawned, absorbing label descent locally and allowing a trusted sanitizer to return a bounded derivative to the unchanged parent. Governed by a two-monoid model over security labels and shared event logs, we formally prove parent label preservation and merge confinement. Finally, we evaluate APPA on a multi-turn tool-chaining benchmark across four models: it suppresses exfiltration (31%-50% down to 0%-7% attack success), and on three of the four, branching recovers a substantial share of the utility that taint tracking alone forfeits.

</details>


### [310] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Symbolic Security Protocol Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20712)
*Paolo Modesti,Syed Ahmed,Ioannis Sfyrakis,Derek Enodolomwanyi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了GPT和DeepSeek在聊天和推理模式下对130个混淆AnB/AnBx协议（388个安全目标）的验证能力，与ProVerif和OFMC对比。聊天模型召回率69-81%但精度低于31%，推理模型精度达66.5%（GPT）和45.4%（DeepSeek）但仅检测到一半以上的攻击。所有模型在认证目标上表现最差，保密性F1最高达95.7%。模型输出不稳定（GPT 89.7%、DeepSeek 74.0%一致），自述置信度与正确性无相关性。LLM目前无法匹配形式化验证工具，最多可作为预筛选过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证工具（如ProVerif、OFMC）在安全协议分析中可靠但需要专业知识和计算资源。大语言模型（LLM）在多种推理任务中表现出色，但能否用于安全协议验证尚不清楚。本文旨在系统评估LLM在安全协议验证中的能力边界，探讨其是否可作为传统形式化工具的替代或补充。

Method: 使用130个混淆的AnB/AnBx协议（388个安全目标），以ProVerif和OFMC的验证结果作为基准。测试GPT和DeepSeek的聊天模式和推理模式，每模型每协议运行三轮，评估召回率、精度、F1分数，并分析不同安全目标类型（认证、保密性等）的表现差异及输出稳定性。

Result: 聊天模式：召回率69-81%，精度<31%；推理模式：GPT精度66.5%，DeepSeek精度45.4%，但仅检测到一半以上攻击。DeepSeek两模式对比（同一底层模型）显示推理将精度从27.2%提升至45.4%。认证目标最差（推理模型检测不到一半的注入和非注入一致性攻击）；保密性最佳（推理模式F1达95.7%）。输出稳定性：GPT 89.7%一致，DeepSeek 74.0%一致。自述置信度与正确性无相关。

Conclusion: LLM在安全协议验证上目前无法匹配形式化验证工具。其最佳用途可能是作为预筛选过滤器（高召回场景用聊天模式，高精度场景用推理模式），但输出不稳定和置信度不可靠是主要局限。尤其是在认证目标的验证上表现严重不足，不能取代传统形式化方法。

Abstract: Security protocol verification relies on formal tools such as ProVerif and OFMC. This study evaluates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can perform comparable analysis. We test GPT and DeepSeek in chat and reasoning modes over three runs on 130 obfuscated AnB/AnBx protocols covering 388 security goals, scored against ProVerif and OFMC. Chat models reach 69 to 81% recall at precision below 31%. Reasoning models reverse this trade-off, reaching 66.5% precision for GPT and 45.4% for DeepSeek, but detect just over half the attacks. DeepSeek's two modes share one underlying model, so the comparison isolates reasoning itself, which raises precision from 27.2% to 45.4%. The GPT contrast spans a model-version change and is only suggestive. All models perform worst on authentication goals: reasoning models detect well under half of injective and non-injective agreement attacks, whereas chat models over-flag them at low precision. Confidentiality is the exception, with F1 up to 95.7% in reasoning mode. Verdicts are unstable across runs, identical on 89.7% of goals for GPT but 74.0% for DeepSeek. Self-reported confidence is uniformly high yet shows no meaningful correlation with correctness. On this benchmark LLMs do not match formal verification, but may serve, at best, as pre-screening filters.

</details>
